const MKTAB=(function(){
'use strict';
const NL=[2048,2048,2048,1024,1024,1024,1024,1024,1024,1024,1024],HL=[512,256,128,64,32,16,8,4,2,1,1];
const OFF=[];let SET=0;for(const n of NL){OFF.push(SET);SET+=n+1;}
const RS=[1,2,1.49831],HZ=220.27,KMAX=512;
const GRP=[[1,7,-12,'Sinus'],[8,14,-9,'Bass'],[15,23,-9,'Welle'],[24,26,-9,'Quinte'],[27,34,-6,'Digital'],[35,35,-8,'Endlos'],
[36,39,-12,'E-Piano'],[40,46,-12,'Orgel'],[47,48,-9,'Clavi'],[49,51,-9,'Gitarre'],[52,56,-9,'Bass II'],[57,60,-9,'Glocke'],[61,64,-12,'Stimme']];
const grpOf=n=>GRP.find(g=>n>=g[0]&&n<=g[1])||GRP[0];
const isOct=n=>n===35||n>=61;
const OCTF=[];for(let j=0;j<8;j++)OCTF.push(440*Math.pow(2,(24+12*j-69)/12));
const E35=[-2.0,-1.7,-0.1,0,-0.6,-0.7,-4.1];
let D=null;
function b64(s){
if(typeof atob==='function'){const t=atob(s),u=new Uint8Array(t.length);for(let i=0;i<t.length;i++)u[i]=t.charCodeAt(i);return u;}
return new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(s,'base64'));
}
function data(){
if(D)return D;
const b=b64(MK_WD);D=[null];if(!b.length)return D;
const salt=b[0];let i=1;
for(let n=1;n<=64;n++){
const h=b[i++]^salt,r=RS[h&3],K=32*(((h>>2)&3)+1),a=new Float64Array(K+1),p=new Float64Array(K+1);
for(let k=1;k<=K;k++){
const v=((b[i]^salt)<<16)|((b[i+1]^salt)<<8)|(b[i+2]^salt);i+=3;
const cl=v>>12,cp=v&4095;
a[k]=cl?Math.pow(10,-(4095-cl)*.025/20):0;p[k]=cp*2*Math.PI/4096;
}
D.push({r,K,a,p});
}
return D;
}
const schroeder=k=>-Math.PI*k*(k-1)/1024;
function abar(w){let e=0,c=0;for(let k=Math.round(16*w.r)+1;k<=Math.round(32*w.r);k++){e+=w.a[k]*w.a[k];c++;}return Math.sqrt(e/Math.max(1,c));}
function harm(n,f){
const w=data()[n],A=new Float64Array(KMAX+1),P=new Float64Array(KMAX+1);if(!w)return{A,P};
const s=grpOf(n)[2],ab=abar(w),roll=ab>=Math.pow(10,-50/20);
if(isOct(n)){
const F=f||HZ,e=env(n);
for(let k=1;k<=KMAX;k++){const a=envA(e,k,F);if(a||n!==35){A[k]=a;P[k]=e.P[k];}}
return{A,P};
}
for(let k=1;k<=KMAX;k++){
if(k<=w.K){A[k]=w.a[k];P[k]=w.p[k];}
else{A[k]=roll?ab*Math.pow(k/(22.6*w.r),s/6.02):0;P[k]=schroeder(k);}
}
return{A,P};
}
const ENV={};
function env(n){
if(ENV[n])return ENV[n];const w=data()[n];if(!w||!isOct(n))return null;
const P=new Float64Array(KMAX+1);for(let k=1;k<=KMAX;k++)P[k]=k<=w.K?w.p[k]:schroeder(k);
if(n===35)return(ENV[n]={t:2,E:E35.slice(),P,r:w.r});
const Ld=new Float64Array(w.K+1);for(let k=1;k<=w.K;k++)Ld[k]=w.a[k]>0?20*Math.log10(w.a[k]):-100;
const ab=abar(w);
return(ENV[n]={t:1,K:w.K,Ld,ab,s:grpOf(n)[2],roll:ab>=Math.pow(10,-50/20)?1:0,hz:HZ,P,r:w.r});
}
function envA(e,k,F){
if(e.t===2){
if(k&(k-1))return 0;
const x=k*F/2,o=Math.log2(x/110),E=e.E;let L;
if(o<=0)L=E[0]+6*o;else if(o>=6)L=E[6]-8*(o-6);else{const j=Math.floor(o),t=o-j;L=E[j]+(E[j+1]-E[j])*t;}
return Math.pow(10,L/20);
}
const h=k*F/e.hz,Ld=e.Ld,K=e.K;
if(h<=1)return Math.pow(10,(Ld[1]-12*Math.log2(1/h))/20);
if(h<=K){const m=Math.min(K-1,Math.floor(h)),t=Math.log(h/m)/Math.log((m+1)/m);return Math.pow(10,(Ld[m]+(Ld[m+1]-Ld[m])*t)/20);}
return e.roll?e.ab*Math.pow(h/22.6,e.s/6.02):0;
}
function envMsg(n){
const e=env(n);if(!e)return null;
const g=n===35?[norm(35,HZ)]:OCTF.map(f=>norm(n,f));
return Object.assign({},e,{E:e.E?e.E.slice():null,Ld:e.Ld?e.Ld.slice():null,P:e.P.slice(),g,f0:OCTF[0]});
}
const TW={};
function tw(n){let t=TW[n];if(t)return t;t={c:new Float64Array(n/2),s:new Float64Array(n/2)};for(let k=0;k<n/2;k++){t.c[k]=Math.cos(2*Math.PI*k/n);t.s[k]=Math.sin(2*Math.PI*k/n);}return(TW[n]=t);}
function fft(re,im,sign){
const n=re.length,T=tw(n);
for(let i=1,j=0;i<n;i++){let b=n>>1;for(;j&b;b>>=1)j^=b;j^=b;if(i<j){let x=re[i];re[i]=re[j];re[j]=x;x=im[i];im[i]=im[j];im[j]=x;}}
for(let len=2;len<=n;len<<=1){
const h=len>>1,st=n/len;
for(let i=0;i<n;i+=len)for(let j=0;j<h;j++){
const c=T.c[j*st],s=sign*T.s[j*st],k=i+j,l=k+h,tr=re[l]*c-im[l]*s,ti=re[l]*s+im[l]*c;
re[l]=re[k]-tr;im[l]=im[k]-ti;re[k]+=tr;im[k]+=ti;
}
}
}
function level(H,L){
const N=NL[L],K=Math.min(HL[L],N/2-1),re=new Float64Array(N),im=new Float64Array(N);
for(let k=1;k<=K;k++){const a=H.A[k];if(!a)continue;re[k]=a*Math.sin(H.P[k]);im[k]=-a*Math.cos(H.P[k]);}
fft(re,im,1);return re;
}
const NORM={};
function peak0(n,f){const x=level(harm(n,f),0);let p=0;for(let i=0;i<x.length;i++){const a=Math.abs(x[i]);if(a>p)p=a;}return p;}
function norm(n,f){
const key=n+'|'+(n>=61?f:0);if(NORM[key])return NORM[key];
const p=peak0(n,n===35?HZ:f);
return(NORM[key]=p>0?1/p:1);
}
function range(n,f,oct){
if(!isOct(n)||oct==null)return[0,10];
const r=data()[n].r,q=x=>Math.log2(x/r*512/11025);
const lo=oct===0?0:Math.max(0,Math.ceil(q(f/2)-2.13)-1),hi=oct===7?10:Math.min(10,Math.ceil(q(2*f))+1);
return[lo,Math.max(lo+1,hi)];
}
function builder(spec){
const n=spec.n|0,oct=isOct(n)&&spec.oct!=null?spec.oct:null,f=isOct(n)?(spec.f||(oct!=null?OCTF[oct]:HZ)):0;
const[lo,hi]=range(n,f,oct),buf=new Float32Array(SET),out=NL.map((N,L)=>buf.subarray(OFF[L],OFF[L]+N+1));
let H=null,g=0,L=-1;
return{lo,hi,out,buf,
step(){
if(L<0){H=harm(n,f);L=lo;return false;}
if(L>hi)return true;
const N=NL[L],t=out[L];
if(L===10){const a=out[9],M=a.length-1;for(let i=0;i<N;i++)t[i]=a[(i*M/N)|0];}
else{
const x=level(H,L);
if(!g){const key=n+'|'+(n>=61?f:0);
if(NORM[key])g=NORM[key];else if(L===0&&n!==35){let p=0;for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const v=Math.abs(x[i]);if(v>p)p=v;}g=NORM[key]=p>0?1/p:1;}else g=norm(n,f);}
for(let i=0;i<N;i++)t[i]=x[i]*g;
}
t[N]=t[0];L++;return L>hi;
}};
}
function mkTables(spec){const B=builder(spec);while(!B.step());return B.out;}
function wave(n){
if(!isOct(n))return{n,r:data()[n]?data()[n].r:1,o:0,d:mkTables({n})[0].buffer,R:[[0,10]]};
const d=new Float32Array(SET*8),R=[];
for(let j=0;j<8;j++){const B=builder({n,oct:j});while(!B.step());d.set(B.buf,j*SET);R.push([B.lo,B.hi]);}
return{n,r:data()[n].r,o:1,d:d.buffer,R,e:envMsg(n)};
}
return{mkTables,builder,range,wave:n=>Object.assign(wave(n),{L:NL}),harm,data,norm,isOct,env,envA,envMsg,r:n=>(data()[n]?data()[n].r:1),group:n=>{const g=grpOf(n);return[g[3],n-g[0]+1,g[2]];},
NL,HL,OFF,SET,OCTF,HZ,GRP,ready:()=>data().length>1};
})();
/*}MKT*/



function mkVowel(o,W){
o=o||{};W=W||(typeof SQMK!=='undefined'?SQMK.W():null);if(!W)return new Float32Array(0);
const sr=o.sr||48000,n=Math.round((o.sec||1)*sr),f0=o.f0||110,seq=Array.isArray(o.v)?o.v:[o.v||'a'],seg=o.seg||1e9;
const out=new Float32Array(n),oq=W.vowOQ,tp=.714*oq,tn=.286*oq,lp=1-Math.exp(-2*Math.PI*300/sr),B=W.vowB,G=W.vowG;
const S=[0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0];let ph=0,z1=0,z2=0,dc=0,xp=0,F=W.VOW[seq[0]]||W.VOW.a,Fs=F.slice();
const gl=1-Math.exp(-1/(.012*sr));
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
const v=seq[Math.min(seq.length-1,Math.floor(i/sr/seg))%seq.length];F=W.VOW[v]||F;
const f=f0*Math.pow(2,(o.vib||0)/1200*Math.sin(2*Math.PI*(o.vibHz||5.5)*i/sr));
ph+=f/sr;if(ph>=1)ph-=1;
let y=ph<tp? .5-.5*Math.cos(Math.PI*ph/tp):ph<oq?Math.cos(.5*Math.PI*(ph-tp)/tn):0;
dc+=(y-dc)*.0005;y-=dc;z1+=lp*(y-z1);z2+=lp*(z1-z2);
let x=z2;
if(!o.raw)for(let k=0;k<4;k++){
Fs[k]+=(F[k]-Fs[k])*gl;
const g=Math.tan(Math.PI*Math.min(Fs[k],.45*sr)/sr),q=Fs[k]/B[k],kk=1/q,a1=1/(1+g*(g+kk)),a2=g*a1,a3=g*a2,j=2*k;
const v3=x-S[j+1],v1=a1*S[j]+a2*v3,v2=S[j+1]+a2*S[j]+a3*v3;S[j]=2*v1-S[j];S[j+1]=2*v2-S[j+1];x=v2*G[k];
}
out[i]=x-xp;xp=x;
}
if(o.rms!=null){let e=0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)e+=out[i]*out[i];const r=Math.sqrt(e/Math.max(1,n)),g=r>0?Math.pow(10,o.rms/20)/r:0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)out[i]*=g;}
return out;
}

tab:'geraet',land:false,ansicht:'ganz',haelfte:'links',halt:0,letzterHalt:1,spielHaelfte:0,run:0,runN:0,orig:null,origKey:'',lastKnob:null,hint:{},bank:0,

vefs:K('FOLGER','voc.efs',{f:v=>(v>=127?['HLd','127 = HALTEN (Formant eingefroren)']:[z3(v),'Abfall '+sec(W().vocTau(v))+' · Anstieg '+sec(Math.max(.001,W().vocTau(v)/4))])}),

q1:K('QUELLE 1 ▾','in.e1',{src:'e1'}),q2:K('QUELLE 2 ▾','in.e2',{src:'e2'}),
pvcut:K('CUTOFF','voc.cut',{f:v=>[z3(v),ft(v,1)+' · Träger-Bänder']}),pvres:K('RESONANZ','voc.res',{f:v=>[z3(v),qTxt(v)]}),
pvatk:K('ATTACK','pvA',{f:v=>fTime('a',v)}),pvrel:K('RELEASE','pvR',{f:v=>fTime('r',v)}),
});
for(let c=0;c<16;c++)Object.assign(KN,{['ch'+c]:K('KANAL '+(c+1),'voc.chl.'+c,{ch:c,f:v=>[z3(v),'Band '+(c+1)+' · '+(v<=0?'aus':dbT(20*Math.log10(W().vocChl(v))))]}),
['cp'+c]:K('KANAL '+(c+1),'voc.chp.'+c,{ch:c,f:v=>[z3(v),'Band '+(c+1)+' · '+(Math.round(v)===0?'Mitte':(v<0?'L':'R')+Math.abs(Math.round(v)))]})});

L_.mode4=[['Einzeln','Dual','Split','Vocoder'],['SGL','DUL','SPL','VOC']];
Object.assign(KN,{
mode4:{l:'MODUS',p:'mode4',L:'mode4'},arpOn:K('AN','arp.on',{L:'onoff'}),
kbOct:{l:'OKTAVE',p:'kbOct',f:v=>[ft(v,1),(Math.round(v)>0?'+':'')+Math.round(v)+' · Tastatur ab C'+(Math.round(v)+3)]},
jump:{l:'SEITEN-SPRUNG',p:'jump',L:'onoff',f:v=>{const k=Math.round(v)?1:0;return[L_.onoff[1][k],k?'an · die Anzeige springt zum gedrehten Regler':'aus · die Anzeige bleibt auf der Seite'];}},
trim1:{l:'E1-PEGEL',p:'trim1',f:v=>[z3(v),trimTxt(v)]},trim2:{l:'E2-PEGEL',p:'trim2',f:v=>[z3(v),trimTxt(v)]},
g1:K('EINGANG 1','in.e1',{src:'e1'}),g2:K('EINGANG 2','in.e2',{src:'e2'})});

const trimDb=v=>(v<=0?-Infinity:v<=100?-40+40*(v-1)/99:6*(v-100)/27);
const trimTxt=v=>(v<=0?'stumm':dbT(trimDb(v)))+' · Eingang (nicht gespeichert)';
function trimGet(w){const tr=S.tracks[P.i],o=tr&&R.mkInTrim&&R.mkInTrim[tr.uid];return o&&o[w]!=null?o[w]:100;}
function trimSet(w,v){const tr=S.tracks[P.i];if(!tr||!tr.uid)return;if(!R.mkInTrim)R.mkInTrim={};const o=R.mkInTrim[tr.uid]||(R.mkInTrim[tr.uid]={e1:100,e2:100});o[w]=clamp(v,0,127);try{mkInTrimApply(P.i);}catch(e){logErr(e);}}
const stR=s=>{const tg=Math.round(ct().seq.q[s].k);return tg?sqR(tg):63;};
const STD=[];
function stepDef(s,n){const k=s*16+n;return STD[k]||(STD[k]={l:'SEQ'+(s+1)+' S'+String(n+1).padStart(2,'0'),p:'t.0.seq.q.'+s+'.v.'+n,sst:n,sqs:s,f:v=>[ft(v,1),stepTxt(s,v)]});}
function stepTxt(s,v){const tg=Math.round(ct().seq.q[s].k);return(Math.round(v)>0?'+':'')+Math.round(v)+(tg?' · '+L_.sqtg[0][tg]+' (±'+sqR(tg)+')':' · ZIEL fehlt (Seite '+(19+s)+')');}
const snd=()=>sndOf(P.i),mk=()=>snd().p.mk;
const voc=()=>mk().mode===3;
const dualOn=()=>{const m=mk().mode;return m===1||m===2;};
const ti=()=>(P.tim===2&&dualOn()?1:0),ct=()=>mk().t[ti()];
const tp=(p,k)=>(p&&p.startsWith('t.0.')?'t.'+(k==null?ti():k)+p.slice(3):p);
const tLab=()=>(dualOn()?'T'+(ti()+1)+' · ':'');
const VIEWP=/^(mode4|bpm|kbOct|jump|trim[12])$/;
function get(d){
const s_=snd();if(!d||!d.p||!s_||!s_.p.mk)return 0;
switch(d.p){
case'mode4':return s_.p.mk.mode;
case'mw':return Math.round((+s_.p.mw||0)*127);
case'bpm':return S.bpm;
case'vol':return Math.round(s_.fx.vol*127);
case'kbOct':return((S.tracks[P.i]&&S.tracks[P.i].kbOct)||4)-4;
case'jump':return P.jump;
case'trim1':return trimGet('e1');
case'trim2':return trimGet('e2');
}
if(d.src)return srcIdx(d.src);
if(d.wv)return Math.floor(s_.p.mk.t[ti()].o1.c2/2)+1;
{const o=kv1Ov(P.i,'mk.'+tp(d.p));if(o!==undefined)return o;}
const v=SQMK.mkPathGet(s_.p.mk,tp(d.p));return typeof v==='number'?v:0;
}
function range(d){
if(!d||!d.p)return[0,1];
if(d.L)return[0,L_[d.L][0].length-1];
if(d.wv)return[1,64];
if(d.sst!=null){const R_=stR(d.sqs);return[-R_,R_];}
switch(d.p){case'mw':case'vol':case'trim1':case'trim2':return[0,127];case'bpm':return[40,240];case'kbOct':return[-3,3];}
if(d.src)return[0,Math.max(1,srcList().length-1)];
const r=SQMK.mkRange(d.p);return r?[r[0],r[1]]:[0,127];
}
function setv(d,v,live){
const i=P.i,s_=snd();if(!d||!d.p||!s_)return;
switch(d.p){
case'mw':setP(i,'mw',clamp(v,0,127)/127);mkWheelSync(i,true);return;
case'bpm':setBpm(Math.round(v));return;
case'vol':setP(i,'fx.vol',clamp(v,0,127)/127);return;
case'kbOct':{const tr=S.tracks[i];const o=clamp(4+Math.round(v),1,7);if(o!==(tr.kbOct||4)){tr.kbOct=o;markDirty();UI.kbRelayout=true;UI.dirty.kb=true;}return;}
case'jump':P.jump=Math.round(v)?1:0;return;
case'trim1':trimSet('e1',v);return;
case'trim2':trimSet('e2',v);return;
case'mode4':{const m=clamp(Math.round(v),0,3),was=s_.p.mk.mode;if(m===was)return;if(m===3&&P.rec)recCancel('abgebrochen (Vocoder hat keinen Mod-Sequenzer)');mkSetP(i,'mode',m,false);if(m===3)mkVocAutoMic(i);
if(!(m===1||m===2))P.tim=1;if((was===3)!==(m===3)){P.seqSel=0;P.chSel=0;if(P.view)viewClose();}return;}
}
if(d.src)return;
if(d.wv){mkSetP(i,tp(d.p),2*(clamp(Math.round(v),1,64)-1),false);return;}
if(d.sst!=null){const R_=stR(d.sqs);mkSetP(i,tp(d.p),clamp(Math.round(v),-R_,R_),false);return;}
mkSetP(i,tp(d.p),v,live);
}
function fmtv(d,v){
if(!d)return['',''];
if(d.src){const L=srcList(),x=L[clamp(Math.round(v),0,L.length-1)]||L[0];return[x.code,x.name];}
if(d.f)return d.f(v);
if(d.L){const L=L_[d.L],k=clamp(Math.round(v),0,L[0].length-1);return[L[1][k],L[0][k]];}
if(d.wv){const n=clamp(Math.round(v),1,64);return[z3(n),wvName(n)+wvM(n)+' · nachgezeichnet'];}
if(d.lfo&&/note/.test(d.p)){const k=clamp(Math.round(v),0,14),t=W().LSYNC_T[k];return[t.replace('/','.'),t+' (Song-Tempo)'];}
return[z3(v),String(Math.round(v))];
}
function lcdVal(d){
const v=get(d);
if(d.src){const L=srcList(),x=L[clamp(Math.round(v),0,L.length-1)];return x?x.name:'aus';}
if(d.p==='voc.efs'&&v>=127)return'HOLD';
if(d.L){const k=clamp(Math.round(v),0,L_[d.L][0].length-1);return L_[d.L][0][k];}
if(d.wv){const n=clamp(Math.round(v),1,64);return'W'+n+(n===60?'≈':'');}
if(d.lfo&&/note/.test(d.p))return W().LSYNC_T[clamp(Math.round(v),0,14)];
if(d.p==='arp.range')return String(Math.round(v));
if(/^eq\.(lf|hf)$/.test(d.p))return hz(v).replace(' ','').replace(',','.');
if(/^eq\.(lg|hg)$/.test(d.p))return db3(v);
const[lo]=range(d),x=Math.round(v);return lo<0?(x>0?'+':'')+x:String(x).padStart(3,'0');
}
const lcdNm=d=>(d.lcd||d.l||'').replace(' ▾','');
function lcdPair(name,val){val=String(val);let n=name;const room=16-val.length-1;if(n.length>room)n=n.slice(0,Math.max(4,room));const s=n+' '.repeat(Math.max(1,16-n.length-val.length))+val;return s.length>16?s.slice(0,16):s;}
const pad16=s=>{s=String(s);return s.length>=16?s.slice(0,16):s+' '.repeat(16-s.length);};
function sqShadow(k){const q=ct().seq.q,tg=Math.round(q[k].k);if(!tg)return-1;for(let j=2;j>k;j--)if(Math.round(q[j].k)===tg)return j;return-1;}
const sqR=tg=>W().sqR(tg);
function seqTxt(){const S_=ct().seq,q=S_.q[P.sq],tg=Math.round(q.k);
return(S_.on?'an':'aus')+' · SEQ '+(P.sq+1)+' → '+L_.sqtg[0][tg]+(tg?' (±'+sqR(tg)+')':'')+(sqShadow(P.sq)>=0?' *':'')+' · '+L_.sqm[0][q.m]+' · '+S_.last+' Schritte · '+L_.sres[0][S_.res]+' · '+L_.sqty[0][S_.type]+' · '+L_.sqrun[0][S_.run];}
const O1C=[['FORM','WFM'],['BREITE','PWM'],['FORM','WFM'],['X-MOD','X-LFO'],['FORM','WFM'],['–','WELLE'],['CUTOFF','RESO.'],['BALANCE','BAL-LFO']];
function lcdName(d){
if(d&&(d.p==='t.0.o1.c1'||d.p==='t.0.o1.c2'))return d.l+' ('+(voc()&&ct().o1.w===3?['FORM','WFM']:O1C[clamp(ct().o1.w,0,7)])[d.p.endsWith('c1')?0:1]+')';

}
function secName(){const pg=curPage();return P.mode==='edit'&&pg?pg[1]:P.mode==='global'?'GLOBAL':(P.lastSec||'GERÄT');}
const wvM=n=>(n===60?' ≈':'');
function wvName(n){const g=MKTAB.group(n);return'Speicherwelle '+n+' · '+g[0]+(MKTAB.GRP.find(x=>x[3]===g[0])[1]>MKTAB.GRP.find(x=>x[3]===g[0])[0]?' '+g[1]:'');}
function fxTxt(pos){
const m=mk(),f=(k,v)=>fmtv(KN[k],v)[1];
if(pos===4)return m.mfx.dep?L_.mfx[0][m.mfx.type]+' · TEMPO '+hz(W().fxHz(m.mfx.spd))+' · TIEFE '+m.mfx.dep:L_.mfx[0][m.mfx.type]+' · aus (TIEFE 0)';

function srcList(){
const i=P.i,L=[{v:null,code:'OFF',name:'aus'}];
if(micAvail())L.push({v:'mic',code:'nIC',name:'Mikrofon'+(vocMicOn()?'':' (Tipp startet)')});
S.tracks.forEach((t,j)=>{if(!t||j===i||!t.uid)return;L.push({v:'t:'+t.uid,code:'t '+(j+1),name:'Spur '+(j+1)+' · '+t.name,j,cyc:mkInCycle(i,j)});});
return L;
}
function srcIdx(w){const m=S.tracks[P.i]&&S.tracks[P.i].mkIn,v=m?m[w]:null,L=srcList(),k=L.findIndex(x=>x.v===v);return k<0?0:k;}

function srcPop(anchor,d){

openPop(anchor,p=>{

for(const x of srcList())h+=`<button class="rb${x.v===cur?' on':''}"${x.cyc?' disabled':''} data-v="${esc(x.v||'')}"><b>${esc(x.name)}</b>${x.cyc?'<small>nicht möglich (Kreis)</small>':x.v==='mic'?'<small>'+(vocMicOn()?'läuft':'startet mit dem Tipp')+' · nichts wird aufgenommen'+'</small>':x.j!=null?'<small>vor dem Regler</small>':''}</button>`;
if(vocMicOn())h+='<button class="rb" data-a="micoff"><b>Mikrofon stoppen</b></button>';
h+=`<button class="rb${dry?' on':''}" data-a="dry"><b>Quelle nur als Eingang: ${dry?'an':'aus'}</b><small>an = die Quelle nur noch hier hören</small></button></div>`;
p.innerHTML=h;guardTouch(p);
const Ls=p.querySelector('.plist.mksrc');if(Ls)Ls.addEventListener('touchstart',ev=>ev.stopPropagation(),{passive:true});

closePop();const i=P.i;
if(b.dataset.a==='micoff'){vocMicStop();renderSoon();lcdShow('MIKROFON','aus – freigegeben');return;}
if(b.dataset.a==='dry'){mkInSet(i,w,undefined,!dry?0:1);renderSoon();lcdShow(d.l,'Quelle nur als Eingang: '+(!dry?'an':'aus'));return;}
const v=b.dataset.v||null;
if(!mkInSet(i,w,v)){lcdShow(d.l,'nicht wählbar');return;}
if(v==='mic'&&!vocMicOn())vocMicStart().then(ok=>{renderSoon();lcdShow(d.l,ok?'Mikrofon läuft · Stopp in dieser Liste':'Mikrofon nicht verfügbar');});
renderSoon();lcdShow(secName()+' · '+d.l,srcTxt(w));dispShow(fmtv(d,get(d))[0]);
hint('in','EINGÄNGE','E1 Instrument (Träger), E2 Stimme · Mikrofon ≈ 70 ms: Kabel-Kopfhörer');
},'button[data-v],button[data-a]');
},{name:'mksrc'});
}
function holdGrab(i){const E=AU.synth[i];if(!E||E.dead||!E.mkHoldGet)return Promise.resolve(null);return Promise.race([E.mkHoldGet(),new Promise(r=>setTimeout(()=>r(null),600))]);}
const holdOn=()=>{const v=mk().voc;return!!v.hold||v.efs>=127;};
async function holdToggle(before){
const i=P.i,V=mk().voc;
if(holdOn()&&!before){
soundEdit(i,MK_LABEL+' Formant halten aus',()=>{mkSetP(i,'voc.hold',null);if(V.efs>=127)mkSetP(i,'voc.efs',P.efsPrev!=null&&P.efsPrev<127?P.efsPrev:64,false);});
haptic('tick');renderSoon();lcdShow('FORMANT HALTEN','aus – der Folger läuft wieder');return;
}
const b=before||soundSnapshot(i),vals=await holdGrab(i);
if(P.i!==i||!sndOf(i)||!sndOf(i).p.mk)return;
if(!vals){if(before)commitSound(i,b,MK_LABEL+' FOLGER');renderSoon();lcdShow('FORMANT HALTEN','nur mit Studio-Klang');return;}
mkSetP(i,'voc.hold',vals);commitSound(i,b,MK_LABEL+' Formant halten');haptic('tick');renderSoon();
lcdShow('FORMANT HALTEN',vals.some(x=>x>0)?'an – der Vokal bleibt, auch ohne Mikrofon':'an – aber an E2 kam nichts: still');
}

}

const ACTS={init:'INIT',tneu:'TIMBRE NEU',tcopy:'TIMBRE KOPIEREN ▸',tswap:'TIMBRE TAUSCHEN',sinit:'SEQ-INIT',scopy:'SEQ KOPIEREN ▸',sswap:'SEQ TAUSCHEN ▸',
werk:'WERKSKLANG ZURÜCK',chl:'PEGEL ZURÜCK',chp:'PAN ZURÜCK'};
const VIEWS={eigene:'EIGENE ▸',schritte:'SCHRITTE ▸',baender:'16 BÄNDER ▸',name:'NAME'};
const pages=()=>(P.mode==='global'?PGL:voc()?PV:PS);
function curPage(){const L=pages(),k=P.mode==='global'?P.gPage:P.page;return L[clamp(k,0,L.length-1)];}
function itemDef(x){
if(x[0]==='!')return{act:x.slice(1),l:ACTS[x.slice(1)]};
if(x[0]==='@')return{view:x.slice(1),l:VIEWS[x.slice(1)]};
if(x==='c2*')return ct().o1.w===5?KN.c2w:KN.c2;
if(x==='l1f*'||x==='l2f*'){const n=x[1];return ct()['l'+n].sync?KN['l'+n+'n']:KN['l'+n+'f'];}
if(x==='dly*')return mk().dly.sync?KN.dlyN:KN.dlyZ;
return KN[x];
}
const curItems=()=>(curPage()?curPage()[3]:[]);
function curItem(){const L=curItems();P.cur=clamp(P.cur,0,Math.max(0,L.length-1));return itemDef(L[P.cur]);}
function pageOf(path){
const L=voc()?PV:PS;
for(let k=0;k<L.length;k++){const it=L[k][3];for(let j=0;j<it.length;j++){const x=it[j],ds=x==='c2*'?[KN.c2,KN.c2w]:x==='l1f*'?[KN.l1f,KN.l1n]:x==='l2f*'?[KN.l2f,KN.l2n]:x==='dly*'?[KN.dlyZ,KN.dlyN]:[itemDef(x)];
for(const d of ds)if(d&&d.p&&d.p===path)return[k,j];}}
return null;
}
function goPage(k,j){const L=pages();if(P.mode==='global')P.gPage=clamp(k,0,L.length-1);else P.page=clamp(k,0,L.length-1);P.cur=j||0;const pg=curPage();const m=/^SEQ (\d)$/.exec(pg[1]);if(m&&!voc())P.sq=+m[1]-1;P.l2=null;renderSoon();}
function pageOfKey(n){const L=voc()?PV:PS;return L.findIndex(p=>p[2]===n);}
function keyOfPage(){if(P.mode!=='edit')return 0;const L=voc()?PV:PS;let k=0;for(let j=0;j<=clamp(P.page,0,L.length-1);j++)if(L[j][2])k=L[j][2];return k;}
function pageDiff(key){
const L=voc()?PV:PS,I0=SQMK.init();let on=false;
for(let j=0;j<L.length;j++){if(L[j][2]&&L[j][2]!==key){if(on)break;continue;}if(L[j][2]===key)on=true;if(!on)continue;
for(const x of L[j][3]){const ds=x==='c2*'?[KN.c2]:x==='l1f*'?[KN.l1f,KN.l1n]:x==='l2f*'?[KN.l2f,KN.l2n]:x==='dly*'?[KN.dlyZ,KN.dlyN]:[itemDef(x)];
for(const d of ds)if(d&&d.p&&!d.src&&!VIEWP.test(d.p)&&d.p!=='mw'&&d.p!=='vol'&&d.p!=='bpm'&&SQMK.mkPathGet(I0,d.p)!==get(d))return true;}}
return false;
}
function lcdLines(){
const s_=snd();if(!s_||!s_.p.mk)return[pad16(''),pad16('')];
const now=performance.now(),cmp=!!(UI.ab&&UI.ab.t===P.i&&UI.ab.side==='B');
if(P.mode==='spiel'){
const lab=mkPanelProg('label')||'---',l1=lab+':'+String(s_.name||'').slice(0,12);
let l2;
if(P.l2&&P.l2.until>now)l2=P.l2.t;
else if(cmp)l2='== COMPARE ==';
else{const md=voc()?'VOCODER':['EINZELN','DUAL','SPLIT'][mk().mode]||'EINZELN',sq=!voc()&&P.seqSel?'SEQ'+P.seqSel+':'+(L_.sqsh[0][Math.round(ct().seq.q[P.seqSel-1].k)]||'–'):voc()&&P.chSel?(P.chSel===1?'CH LEVEL':'CH PAN'):'';
const left=sq||(md+(dualOn()&&P.tim===2?' T2':'')),t='♩='+S.bpm;l2=left.length+1+t.length<=16?left+' '.repeat(16-left.length-t.length)+t:left;}
return[pad16(l1),pad16(l2)];
}
const pg=curPage(),d=curItem(),L=curItems(),letter=String.fromCharCode(65+P.cur);
const l1=pad16(pg[0]+(L.length>1?letter:'')+' '+pg[1]+(P.mode==='edit'&&dualOn()&&P.tim===2?' T2':''));
if(cmp)return[l1,pad16('== COMPARE ==')];
if(P.splitArm)return[l1,pad16('SPLIT ← TASTE ?')];
if(P.l2&&P.l2.until>now&&P.l2.edit)return[l1,pad16(P.l2.t)];
if(!d)return[l1,pad16('')];
if(d.act)return[l1,lcdPair(d.l.replace(' ▸',''),d.l.endsWith('▸')?'▸':'YES?')];
if(d.view==='name')return[l1,pad16(String(s_.name||''))];
if(d.view)return[l1,pad16(d.l)];
return[l1,lcdPair(lcdNm(d),lcdVal(d))];
}
const LCDS={a:'',b:''};
function lcdPaint(){
const[a,b]=lcdLines(),cur=P.mode!=='spiel'&&!(P.l2&&P.l2.until>performance.now());
const key=a+'|'+b+'|'+cur;
for(const lc of[PL.ui&&PL.ui.lcd,P.sp&&P.sp.lcd]){
if(!lc||lc._k===key)continue;lc._k=key;
const ln=lc.children;ln[0].textContent=a;
if(cur&&b.trim()){const m=/^(.*?\S)(\s+)(\S.*)$/.exec(b);if(m){ln[1].innerHTML=esc(m[1]+m[2])+'<span class="cu">'+esc(m[3])+'</span>';continue;}}
ln[1].textContent=b;
}
LCDS.a=a;LCDS.b=b;
}
function showVal(d){
if(!d)return;
if(P.mode==='edit'&&P.jump&&d.p){const pj=pageOf(d.p);if(pj){if(pj[0]!==P.page||pj[1]!==P.cur){P.page=pj[0];P.cur=pj[1];}P.l2=null;}else P.l2={t:lcdPair(lcdNm(d),lcdVal(d)),until:performance.now()+1000,edit:1};}
else if(P.mode==='edit'||P.mode==='global'){const it=curItem();if(!it||it.p!==d.p)P.l2={t:lcdPair(lcdNm(d),lcdVal(d)),until:performance.now()+1000,edit:1};}
else P.l2={t:lcdPair(lcdNm(d),lcdVal(d)),until:performance.now()+1000};
clearTimeout(P.l2T);P.l2T=setTimeout(renderSoon,1020);renderSoon();
}
function lcdOf(d){
if(!d)return;
const v=get(d),[,t]=fmtv(d,v),sec_=d.sst!=null?'SEQ EDIT':secName();
if(d.p==='voc.efs'&&v>=127){lcdShow(sec_+' · '+d.l,'127 = HALTEN (Formant eingefroren)');return;}
if(d.pt!=null){lcdShow(tLab()+patchTxt(d.pt),d.l+' '+(d.L?t:(Math.round(v)>0?'+':'')+Math.round(v)));return;}
const tl=/^(mfx|dly|eq|arp|voc)\./.test(d.p)||VIEWP.test(d.p)||d.p==='vol'?'':tLab(),unit=d.L||d.src||d.nu?'':' '+lcdVal(d);
let extra='';
if(d.p==='t.0.o1.w'&&ct().o1.w===5){const n=Math.floor(ct().o1.c2/2)+1;extra=wvName(n)+wvM(n);}
else if(d.p==='t.0.o1.c1'&&ct().o1.w===5)extra='ohne Wirkung bei der Speicherwelle';
else if((d.p==='tv'||d.p==='split')&&!dualOn())extra='wirkt bei DUAL bzw. SPLIT';
else if(d.p==='split'&&mk().mode===2)extra='Tipp auf die Anzeige = Split ← nächste Note';
else if(d.p==='scale'&&mk().scale===9)extra='EIGENE ▸ auf derselben Seite';
lcdShow(tl+sec_+' · '+lcdName(d)+unit,t+(extra?' · '+extra:''));
if(d.wv||d.p==='t.0.o1.w'&&ct().o1.w===5)hint('wave','SPEICHERWELLE','64 nachgezeichnete Wellen (≈ = Nr. 60, nur grob). Tipp auf die Anzeige = Liste');
}
function hint(k,a,b){if(P.hint[k])return;P.hint[k]=1;lcdShow(a,b);}
function note(s,ms){P.l2={t:pad16(s),until:performance.now()+(ms||1000),edit:1};clearTimeout(P.l2T);P.l2T=setTimeout(renderSoon,(ms||1000)+20);renderSoon();}
const dispShow=()=>{};
function listPop(anchor,d){
const L=L_[d.L],cur=Math.round(get(d));
openPop(anchor,p=>{
const long=L[0].length>12,sqk=d.sqk!=null?d.sqk:-1;
p.innerHTML=`<h4>${esc(secName()+(sqk>=0?' · SEQ '+(sqk+1):'')+' · '+d.l)}</h4><div class="plist mkl${long?' mklong':''}">`+L[0].map((n,k)=>`<button class="rb${k===cur?' on':''}" data-v="${k}">${esc(n)}${d.p==='t.0.o1.w'&&k===7?'<small>E1/E2 (Instrument, Stimme) statt eines Oszillators</small>':d.p==='t.0.o1.w'&&k===5?'<small>64 nachgezeichnete Wellen</small>':d.l==='ZIEL'&&k?'<small>±'+W().sqR(k)+'</small>':''}</button>`).join('')+'</div>';
guardTouch(p);
const Ll=p.querySelector('.plist.mklong'),on_=Ll&&Ll.querySelector('.rb.on');
if(Ll){Ll.addEventListener('touchstart',ev=>ev.stopPropagation(),{passive:true});if(on_)requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{Ll.scrollTop=Math.max(0,on_.offsetTop-Ll.offsetTop-(Ll.clientHeight-on_.offsetHeight)/2);}catch(e){}});}
onTap(p,b=>{closePop();const v=+b.dataset.v;soundEdit(P.i,MK_LABEL+' '+d.l,()=>setv(d,v,false));P.lastKnob=d;renderSoon();lcdOf(d);dispShow(fmtv(d,get(d))[0]);},'button[data-v]');
},{name:'mklist'});
}
function wavePop(anchor,d){
const cur=clamp(Math.round(get(d)),1,64);
wv.all();
openPop(anchor,p=>{
let h=`<h4>${esc('OSZ 1 · STEUER 2 · SPEICHERWELLE')}</h4><p class="small">64 nachgezeichnete Wellen · ≈ = Nr. 60, nur grob nachgezeichnet.</p><div class="plist mkl mkwv">`;
for(const g of MKTAB.GRP){h+=`<h5 class="mkgh">${esc(g[3])}</h5>`;for(let n=g[0];n<=g[1];n++)h+=`<button class="rb${n===cur?' on':''}" data-v="${n}"><b>${n}</b> ${esc(wvName(n).replace(/^Speicherwelle \d+ · /,'')+wvM(n))}</button>`;}
p.innerHTML=h+'</div>';guardTouch(p);
const L=p.querySelector('.plist.mkwv'),on_=p.querySelector('.rb.on');
if(L)L.addEventListener('touchstart',ev=>ev.stopPropagation(),{passive:true});
if(L&&on_)requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{L.scrollTop=Math.max(0,on_.offsetTop-L.offsetTop-(L.clientHeight-on_.offsetHeight)/2);}catch(e){}});
onTap(p,b=>{closePop();const v=+b.dataset.v;soundEdit(P.i,MK_LABEL+' Speicherwelle',()=>setv(d,v,false));P.lastKnob=d;renderSoon();lcdOf(d);dispShow(fmtv(d,get(d))[0]);},'button[data-v]');
},{name:'mkwave'});
}
function timApply(i,label,fn){
const s_=deep(sndOf(i));if(!s_||!s_.p.mk)return;fn(s_.p.mk);s_.p.mk=SQMK.normMk(s_.p.mk);UI.ab=null;
soundEdit(i,MK_LABEL+' '+label,()=>applySound(i,s_));P.lastKnob=null;renderSoon();
}
const tDst=i=>(i==null||i===P.i)&&P.i>=0&&sndOf(P.i)&&sndOf(P.i).p.mk?ti():0;
function timSwap(i){timApply(i,'Timbre tauschen',pm=>{const a=pm.t[0];pm.t[0]=pm.t[1];pm.t[1]=a;});lcdShow('UTILITY · TIMBRE TAUSCHEN','T1 ⇄ T2 getauscht · ↶ = zurück');}
function timInit(i){const k=tDst(i);timApply(i,'Timbre neu',pm=>{pm.t[k]=SQMK.init().t[0];});lcdShow('UTILITY · TIMBRE NEU','T'+(k+1)+' auf INIT-Werte · ↶ = zurück');}
function timCopy(i,src,srcT){
const k=tDst(i),sp=src&&src.p&&src.p.mk?SQMK.normMk(src.p.mk):null;if(!sp)return false;
timApply(i,'Timbre kopieren',pm=>{pm.t[k]=deep(sp.t[srcT?1:0]);});
lcdShow('UTILITY · TIMBRE KOPIEREN',(src.name||'Sound')+' T'+(srcT?2:1)+' → T'+(k+1)+' · ↶ = zurück');return true;
}
function timCopyPop(anchor,t){
const i=t==null?P.i:t,k=tDst(i),L=ownList(),cur=sndOf(i);if(!cur||!cur.p.mk)return;
openPop(anchor,p=>{
let h=`<h4>${esc('Timbre kopieren → T'+(k+1))}</h4><p class="small">Quelle wählen: ein ${esc(MK_LABEL)}-Sound und sein Timbre 1 oder 2.</p><div class="plist mkl mkcp">`;
h+=`<div class="mkcr"><b>${esc(cur.name)} <small>(dieser Sound)</small></b><button class="rb" data-s="-1" data-t="0"${k===0?' disabled':''}>T1</button><button class="rb" data-s="-1" data-t="1"${k===1?' disabled':''}>T2</button></div>`;
L.forEach((r,x)=>{h+=`<div class="mkcr"><b>${esc(r.name)}</b><button class="rb" data-s="${x}" data-t="0">T1</button><button class="rb" data-s="${x}" data-t="1">T2</button></div>`;});
if(!L.length)h+=`<p class="small">Noch keine gesicherten ${esc(MK_LABEL)}-Sounds – mit WRITE sichern, dann stehen sie hier.</p>`;
p.innerHTML=h+'</div>';guardTouch(p);
const Lc=p.querySelector('.plist.mkcp');if(Lc)Lc.addEventListener('touchstart',ev=>ev.stopPropagation(),{passive:true});
onTap(p,b=>{closePop();const x=+b.dataset.s,src=x<0?cur:(SQStore.normalizeSound(deep(L[x]))||L[x]);timCopy(i,src,+b.dataset.t);},'button[data-s]');
},{name:'mkcopy'});
}
function splitArm(){
if(mk().mode!==2){lcdShow('SPLIT','erst 01 COMMON → MODUS auf Split');return;}
P.splitArm=true;UI.mkSplitArm=splitNote;UI.mkSplitOff=splitOff;haptic('tick');
lcdShow('SPLIT','eine Taste spielen = Split-Punkt (darunter T1, ab ihr T2) · jeder andere Tipp oder ■ = aus');renderSoon();
}
function splitOff(quiet){
if(!P.splitArm)return false;P.splitArm=false;UI.mkSplitArm=null;UI.mkSplitOff=null;
if(!quiet)lcdShow('SPLIT','Split ← nächste Note aus');renderSoon();return true;
}
function splitNote(note){
if(!P.splitArm)return false;P.splitArm=false;UI.mkSplitArm=null;UI.mkSplitOff=null;const i=P.i,s_=sndOf(i);if(!s_||!s_.p.mk)return false;
if(R.editor!=='synth'||R.selT!==i||!EL.synthSheet.classList.contains('open'))return false;
note=clamp(Math.round(note),0,127);soundEdit(i,MK_LABEL+' SPLIT',()=>mkSetP(i,'split',note,false));
renderSoon();lcdShow('SPLIT',noteLabel(note)+' · darunter T1, ab hier T2');dispShow(z3(note));return true;
}

const SEC_HALT={ain:1,pwr:1,o1:1,o2:2,mix:3,flt:4,amp:5,eg1:6,eg2:6,l1:7,l2:7,fx:8,arp:9,oct:10,tim:10,msq:10,por:10,sqe:10,bank:10,vp:12,disp:13};
const ELS=EL_.map(([id,art,x,y,t,i,o])=>{
o=o||{};
const X=x*XU,Y=y*YU,sz=art==='k'?[33,33]:art==='b'||art==='bl'?[40,22.6]:art==='s'?[36,36]:art==='l'?[7.5,7.5]
:art==='lcd'?[155,43.6]:art==='name'?[0,0]:[0,0];
const e={id,art,x,y,t:t||null,i:i||null,o,X,Y,w:sz[0],h:sz[1],x0:X-sz[0]/2,y0:Y-sz[1]/2,x1:X+sz[0]/2,y1:Y+sz[1]/2};
e.act=art==='k'||art==='b'||art==='bl'||art==='s'||art==='lcd';

const ELBY=new Map(ELS.map(e=>[e.id,e]));
const ACT=ELS.filter(e=>e.act);
function secOfXY(x,y){for(const s of SEC)if(x>=s[4]&&x<=s[5]&&y>=s[6]-(s[1]?3.2:0)&&y<=s[7])return s[0];return y>82.7&&x>11.6?'steps':y>66&&x>11.6?'knobs':x>=55.8&&x<=77.4&&y<19.1?'arp':null;}
function haltOfEl(e){
const m=/^([ks])(\d+)$/.exec(e.id);
if(m){const n=+m[2];if(m[1]==='s')return n<=8?10:11;return n<=8?6:n<=10?7:n<=12?8:12;}
if(e.id==='name'||e.id==='orig')return 9;
return SEC_HALT[e.sec]||0;
}
const HIT_R=18,HB=20,HBX=Math.ceil(PW/HB),HBY=Math.ceil(PH/HB),BUCK=Array.from({length:HBX*HBY},()=>[]);
for(const e of ACT)for(let cy=Math.max(0,Math.floor((e.y0-HIT_R)/HB));cy<=Math.min(HBY-1,Math.floor((e.y1+HIT_R)/HB));cy++)
for(let cx=Math.max(0,Math.floor((e.x0-HIT_R)/HB));cx<=Math.min(HBX-1,Math.floor((e.x1+HIT_R)/HB));cx++)BUCK[cy*HBX+cx].push(e);
const rdist=(e,x,y)=>{const dx=x<e.x0?e.x0-x:x>e.x1?x-e.x1:0,dy=y<e.y0?e.y0-y:y>e.y1?y-e.y1:0;return Math.hypot(dx,dy);};
function hitAt(x,y,vis){
if(x<0||y<0||x>PW||y>PH)return null;
const c=BUCK[Math.min(HBY-1,Math.floor(y/HB))*HBX+Math.min(HBX-1,Math.floor(x/HB))];let best=null,bd=1e9,bc=1e9;
for(const e of c){
if(vis&&(e.x1<vis[0]||e.x0>vis[2]||e.y1<vis[1]||e.y0>vis[3]))continue;
const d=rdist(e,x,y);if(d>HIT_R)continue;
const dc=Math.hypot(e.X-x,e.Y-y);
if(d<bd-1e-9||(Math.abs(d-bd)<=1e-9&&dc<bc)){best=e;bd=d;bc=dc;}
}
return best;
}
function haltAt(x,y){const px=x/XU,py=y/YU,s=secOfXY(px,py);if(s==='steps')return px<55.6?10:11;if(s==='knobs')return px<55.8?6:px<66.6?7:px<77.7?8:12;if(s==='arp')return 9;return SEC_HALT[s]||0;}
const PL={zb:0,el:null,ui:null,fam:'',mc:null};
function measure(txt,fs,wt){
if(!PL.mc){try{PL.mc=document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');}catch(e){PL.mc=null;}}
if(!PL.mc)return txt.length*fs*.52;
const f=(wt||600)+' '+fs+'px '+PL.fam;if(PL.mf!==f){PL.mf=f;PL.mc.font=f;}
return PL.mc.measureText(txt).width;
}
function fitFs(txt,fs,max,wt){const w=measure(txt,100,wt)/100*fs;return w<=max?fs:Math.max(fs*.55,fs*max/w*.98);}

const NTQ={q:[],ch:null};
function nextTask(f){
if(!NTQ.ch){try{NTQ.ch=new MessageChannel();NTQ.ch.port1.onmessage=()=>{const g=NTQ.q.shift();if(g)g();};}catch(e){NTQ.ch=false;}}
if(!NTQ.ch){setTimeout(f,0);return;}
NTQ.q.push(f);NTQ.ch.port2.postMessage(0);
}
function plateSlice(){
const t0=performance.now();try{tickAhead(.3);}catch(e){}
while(PL.jobs.length&&performance.now()-t0<6)PL.jobs.shift()();
PL.slices.push(+(performance.now()-t0).toFixed(2));PL.ms+=performance.now()-t0;
if(PL.jobs.length){nextTask(plateSlice);return;}
PL.ready=true;PL.done=performance.now()-PL.t0;
const U=P.el;if(U&&U.hull&&U.hull.isConnected&&PL.el.parentNode!==U.hull)U.hull.appendChild(PL.el);
renderSoon();
}
const KDEF={'ain.1':()=>KN.trim1,'ain.2':()=>KN.trim2,vol:()=>KN.vol,
'o1.c1':()=>KN.c1,'o1.c2':()=>(ct().o1.w===5?KN.c2w:KN.c2),
'o2.semi':()=>(voc()?KN.vhpl:KN.semi),'o2.tune':()=>(voc()?KN.vth:KN.o2t),
'mix.o1':()=>KN.m1,'mix.o2':()=>(voc()?KN.vins:KN.m2),'mix.n':()=>KN.mn,
'f.cut':()=>(voc()?KN.vcut:KN.cut),'f.res':()=>(voc()?KN.vres:KN.res),'f.eg':()=>(voc()?KN.vmint:KN.feg),'f.kbd':()=>(voc()?KN.vefs:KN.fkb),
'a.lvl':()=>(voc()?KN.vlvl:KN.lvl),'a.pan':()=>(voc()?KN.vdir:KN.pan),
'arp.tempo':()=>KN.bpm,'arp.gate':()=>KN.arpGate,porta:()=>KN.porta};
function kDef(n){
if(!voc()&&P.seqSel)return stepDef(P.seqSel-1,n-1);
if(voc()&&P.chSel)return KN[(P.chSel===1?'ch':'cp')+(n-1)];
if(n<=8)return KN[(n<=4?'e1':'e2')+'adsr'[(n-1)%4]];
if(n<=10){const l=n===9?1:2;return ct()['l'+l].sync?KN['l'+l+'n']:KN['l'+l+'f'];}
if(n===11)return P.fxSel?(mk().dly.sync?KN.dlyN:KN.dlyZ):KN.mfxS;
if(n===12)return P.fxSel?KN.dlyD:KN.mfxD;
return KN['pi'+(n-13)];
}
function defOf(id){if(/^k\d+$/.test(id))return kDef(+id.slice(1));const f=KDEF[id];return f?f():null;}
const KEYDEF={'o1.wave':()=>KN.o1w,'o2.wave':()=>KN.o2w,'o2.mod':()=>KN.o2m,'f.type':()=>(voc()?KN.vfs:KN.ftp),'a.gate':()=>KN.gate,
'a.dist':()=>(voc()?KN.vdist:KN.dist),'arp.on':()=>KN.arpOn,'arp.latch':()=>KN.arpLat,'arp.range':()=>KN.arpOct,'arp.type':()=>KN.arpTyp,
'l1.sel':()=>KN.l1w,'l2.sel':()=>KN.l2w,'vp.s':()=>(voc()?KN.vmsrc:KN['ps'+P.patchSel]),'vp.d':()=>KN['pd'+P.patchSel],'seq.on':()=>KN.sqOn};
const GRAU={'o2.wave':'OSZ 2 klingt im Vocoder nicht','o2.mod':'OSZ 2 klingt im Vocoder nicht','a.gate':'im Vocoder formt der Träger-Pegel',
tim:'im Vocoder gibt es nur Timbre 1','seq.on':'im Vocoder gibt es keinen Mod-Sequenzer','seq.rec':'im Vocoder gibt es keinen Mod-Sequenzer',
'vp.sel':'im Vocoder gibt es kein VIRTUAL PATCH','vp.d':'im Vocoder gibt es kein VIRTUAL PATCH'};
for(let n=13;n<=16;n++)GRAU['k'+n]='im Vocoder gibt es kein VIRTUAL PATCH (CH PARAM: Kanal '+n+')';
const grau=id=>!!GRAU[id]&&voc()&&!(/^k1[3-6]$/.test(id)&&P.chSel);
function bindOf(id){
const d=defOf(id)||(KEYDEF[id]?KEYDEF[id]():null);
if(d&&d.p)return VIEWP.test(d.p)||d.p==='bpm'||d.p==='vol'||d.p==='mw'||d.src?d.p:tp(d.p);
const V={'fx.sel':'view:fxSel','vp.sel':'view:patchSel','sq.sel':voc()?'view:chSel':'view:seqSel',tim:'view:timbre','oct.d':'track:kbOct','oct.u':'track:kbOct',
'seq.rec':'view:rec','bank.d':'prog:bank','bank.u':'prog:bank',write:'prog:write',no:'lcd:no',yes:'lcd:yes','cur.l':'lcd:cursor','cur.r':'lcd:cursor',
'pg.m':'lcd:page','pg.p':'lcd:page',edit:'lcd:edit',global:'lcd:global',exit:'lcd:exit',lcd:'lcd:tap'};
if(V[id])return V[id];if(/^s\d+$/.test(id))return P.mode==='spiel'?'prog:select':'lcd:page';return null;
}
function ownList(){return(LIB.ok?LIB.lib.all():[]).filter(s=>s.engine==='mk').sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name,'de'));}
function mkPanelProg(action,arg){
const i=P.i,f=window.mkProg;
if(typeof f==='function'){try{const r=f(action,arg,i);if(r!==undefined){renderSoon();return r;}}catch(e){logErr(e);}}
else if(f&&typeof f.select==='function'&&i===R.selT){try{
const c=()=>{const x=f.current();if(x&&x.label!==P.progSeen){P.progSeen=x.label;if(f.mk8&&window.MKF&&MKF.P){MKF.P.bank=x.bank;MKF.P.touched=1;}}if(!x)P.progSeen=null;return x;};
switch(action){
case'label':{const x=c();return x?x.label:'---';}
case'lit':{const x=c();return x&&x.bank===f.bank(0)?x.slot:0;}
case'bank':{c();const b=f.bank(arg);note(lcdPair('BANK','ABCDEFGH'[clamp((b|0)-1,0,7)]||'?'),1200);renderSoon();return;}
case'select':c();f.select(f.bank(0),arg);P.orig=null;renderSoon();return;
case'step':f.step(arg);P.orig=null;renderSoon();return;
case'write':{const a=keyNode('write')||(PL.ui&&PL.ui.lcd&&PL.ui.lcd.isConnected?PL.ui.lcd:null);if(window.MKF&&MKF.savePop)MKF.savePop(a,i);else openSavePop(a,i);hint('write','WRITE','legt den Klang auf einen Platz A01–H16 (Vorgabe: das geladene Programm)');return;}
case'factory':{const x=c();if(x&&x.own){f.restore(x.bank,x.slot);P.orig=null;renderSoon();}else lcdShow('WERKSKLANG ZURÜCK',x?x.label+' spielt schon den Werksklang':'kein Programmplatz geladen');return;}
}}catch(e){logErr(e);}}
const s_=sndOf(i);if(!s_)return undefined;
if(action==='label')return'---';
if(action==='lit'){const L=ownList(),k=L.findIndex(x=>x.id&&(x.id===s_.origin||x.id===s_.id));return k>=P.bank*16&&k<P.bank*16+16?k-P.bank*16+1:0;}
if(action==='bank'){P.bank=clamp(P.bank+arg,0,7);renderSoon();lcdShow('BANK '+(P.bank+1),'eigene Sounds '+(P.bank*16+1)+'–'+(P.bank*16+16)+' auf SELECT 1–16 · Werks-Programme A–H kommen mit dem Programm-Speicher');return;}
if(action==='select'){
const rec=ownList()[P.bank*16+arg-1];
if(!rec){lcdShow('SELECT '+arg,'Platz leer – eigene Sounds dieser Engine sichern (WRITE) füllt die Plätze');return;}
if(rec.id===s_.origin){lcdShow('SELECT '+arg,rec.name+' ist geladen');return;}
UI.ab=null;soundEdit(i,'Programm '+rec.name,()=>loadPreset(i,rec.id));toast('Programm: '+rec.name,{label:'Rückgängig',fn:()=>undo()});P.orig=null;return;
}
if(action==='step'){stepSound(i,arg);return;}
if(action==='write'){openSavePop(keyNode('write')||null,i);hint('write','WRITE','sichert den Sound in der Bibliothek (wie „Sichern“ oben)');return;}
if(action==='factory'){lcdShow('WERKSKLANG ZURÜCK','kommt mit dem Programm-Speicher A–H');return;}
return undefined;
}
const keyNode=id=>(PL.ui&&PL.ui.key[id]&&PL.ui.key[id].isConnected?PL.ui.key[id]:P.sp&&P.sp.keys[id]?P.sp.keys[id]:null);
function cycle(d,label){
const i=P.i,v=Math.round(get(d)),[lo,hi]=range(d);let nv;
if(d.p==='voc.fs'){const O=[0,1,2,-1,-2];nv=O[(O.indexOf(v)+1)%5];}else nv=v+1>hi?lo:v+1;
soundEdit(i,MK_LABEL+' '+(label||d.l),()=>setv(d,nv,false));
P.lastKnob=d;haptic('tick');lcdOf(d);showVal(d);
}
function press(id){
const i=P.i;if(i<0||!sndOf(i)||!sndOf(i).p.mk)return;
if(P.splitArm)splitOff();
firstHint(id);
if(grau(id))lcdShow('VOCODER',GRAU[id]);
if(/^s\d+$/.test(id)){stepKey(+id.slice(1));renderSoon();return;}
if(KEYDEF[id]){cycle(KEYDEF[id]());if(id==='arp.on'&&mk().arp.on)hint('arp','ARPEGGIATOR','Akkord halten – die Töne kommen nacheinander im Song-Tempo · Seite 25 ARPEGGIO');renderSoon();return;}
switch(id){
case'fx.sel':P.fxSel^=1;haptic('tick');lcdShow('EFFECTS','Regler 11 und 12: '+(P.fxSel?'DELAY (TIME, FEEDBACK)':'MOD FX (SPEED, DEPTH)'));break;
case'vp.sel':if(!voc()){P.patchSel=(P.patchSel+1)%4;haptic('tick');lcdShow(tLab()+patchTxt(P.patchSel),'SOURCE und DESTINATION bearbeiten PATCH '+(P.patchSel+1));}break;
case'sq.sel':if(voc()){P.chSel=(P.chSel+1)%3;lcdShow('CH PARAM',P.chSel?'die 16 Regler = Kanal '+(P.chSel===1?'LEVEL':'PAN')+' 1–16':'aus · die Regler bearbeiten wieder ihren Aufdruck');}
else{P.seqSel=(P.seqSel+1)%4;if(P.seqSel)P.sq=P.seqSel-1;lcdShow('SEQ EDIT',P.seqSel?'SEQ '+P.seqSel+': Regler n = Schritt n · '+seqTxt():'aus · die Regler bearbeiten wieder ihren Aufdruck');}
haptic('tick');break;
case'seq.rec':if(!voc())recArm(P.seqSel?P.seqSel-1:0);break;
case'tim':if(voc())break;if(!dualOn()){P.tim=1;lcdShow('TIMBRE SELECT','T2 gibt es bei DUAL oder SPLIT (01 COMMON → MODUS)');break;}
P.tim=P.tim===1?2:1;P.lastKnob=null;haptic('tick');lcdShow('TIMBRE SELECT','die Regler bearbeiten Timbre '+P.tim);break;
case'oct.d':case'oct.u':{const tr=S.tracks[i],o=clamp((tr.kbOct||4)+(id==='oct.u'?1:-1),1,7);if(o!==(tr.kbOct||4)){tr.kbOct=o;markDirty();UI.kbRelayout=true;UI.dirty.kb=true;}
haptic('tick');lcdShow('OCTAVE',(o-4>0?'+':'')+(o-4)+' · Tastatur ab C'+(o-1));break;}
case'bank.d':mkPanelProg('bank',-1);break;case'bank.u':mkPanelProg('bank',1);break;
case'write':mkPanelProg('write');break;
case'edit':P.mode='edit';P.l2=null;hint('edit','EDIT','SELECT 1–16 = Seite, PAGE −/+ blättert, CURSOR wählt den Wert, −/NO +/YES ändern; EXIT = zurück');break;
case'global':P.mode='global';P.l2=null;lcdShow('GLOBAL','1A/1B Eingänge · 1C Oktave · 1D Seiten-Sprung · EXIT = zurück');break;
case'exit':if(P.editHeld>0||(UI.ab&&UI.ab.t===i&&UI.ab.side==='B')){compare();break;}P.mode='spiel';P.l2=null;break;
case'cur.l':case'cur.r':if(P.mode==='spiel'){lcdShow('CURSOR','erst EDIT oder GLOBAL');break;}P.cur=clamp(P.cur+(id==='cur.r'?1:-1),0,curItems().length-1);P.l2=null;break;
case'pg.m':case'pg.p':if(P.mode==='spiel'){P.mode='edit';}goPage((P.mode==='global'?P.gPage:P.page)+(id==='pg.p'?1:-1),0);break;
case'no':case'yes':yesNo(id==='yes'?1:-1);break;
}
renderSoon();
}
function stepKey(n){
if(P.mode==='spiel'){mkPanelProg('select',n);return;}
if(P.mode==='global')P.mode='edit';
const k=pageOfKey(n);if(k>=0)goPage(k,0);haptic('tick');
const pg=curPage();lcdShow('EDIT · '+pg[0]+' '+pg[1],curItems().map(x=>{const d=itemDef(x);return d?lcdNm(d):'';}).join(' · '));
}
function compare(){
const i=P.i,on=!!(UI.ab&&UI.ab.t===i&&UI.ab.side==='B');
const rec=!on&&originRec(sndOf(i)),rs=rec&&(FACTORY_BY_ID.has(rec.id)?factorySound(rec.id):rec);
if(!on&&(!rs||rs.engine!=='mk')){lcdShow('COMPARE','kein gespeicherter '+MK_LABEL+'-Stand zum Vergleichen');return;}
if(!on&&!soundEdited(sndOf(i))){lcdShow('COMPARE','unverändert – nichts zu vergleichen');return;}
toggleAB(i);lcdShow('COMPARE',on?'zurück zu deiner Änderung':'der gespeicherte Stand · EXIT = zurück');
}
const isSnd=d=>d&&d.p&&!d.src&&(!VIEWP.test(d.p)||d.p==='mode4')&&d.p!=='bpm';
function yesNo(dir){
const i=P.i;
if(P.mode==='spiel'){mkPanelProg('step',dir);return;}
const d=curItem();if(!d)return;
if(d.act){if(dir>0)util(d.act);else lcdShow(d.l,'+/YES führt aus');return;}
if(d.view){if(dir>0)lcdTap();return;}
if(d.src){const L=srcList(),k=clamp(srcIdx(d.src)+dir,0,L.length-1),x=L[k];if(x&&!x.cyc){mkInSet(i,d.src,x.v);lcdShow(d.l,srcTxt(d.src));}return;}
const[lo,hi]=range(d),st=d.step||1,v=clamp(Math.round(get(d)/st)*st+dir*st,lo,hi);
if(isSnd(d))soundEdit(i,MK_LABEL+' '+d.l,()=>setv(d,v,false));else setv(d,v,false);
P.lastKnob=d;haptic('tick');lcdOf(d);
}
function util(a){
const i=P.i,anchor=PL.ui&&PL.ui.lcd&&PL.ui.lcd.isConnected?PL.ui.lcd:null;
switch(a){
case'init':initSound(i);P.orig=null;break;
case'tneu':timInit(i);break;case'tcopy':timCopyPop(anchor);break;case'tswap':timSwap(i);break;
case'sinit':seqInit(i,P.seqSel?P.seqSel-1:'all');break;case'scopy':case'sswap':seqToolsPop(anchor);break;
case'chl':chReset('chl');break;case'chp':chReset('chp');break;
case'werk':mkPanelProg('factory');break;
}
}
function lcdTap(){
if(splitOff())return;
const i=P.i,anchor=PL.ui&&PL.ui.lcd&&PL.ui.lcd.isConnected?PL.ui.lcd:P.sp&&P.sp.lcd;
if(P.mode==='spiel'){openBrowser(i);return;}
const d=curItem();if(!d)return;
if(d.view==='name'){openSavePop(anchor,i);return;}
if(d.view){if(d.view==='eigene'&&mk().scale!==9){lcdShow('EIGENE','erst STIMMUNG auf „Eigene“');return;}if(d.view==='baender')P.bA=curPage()[1]==='CH PAN'?'chp':'chl';viewOpen(d.view);return;}
if(d.act){lcdShow(d.l,'+/YES führt aus');return;}
if(d.src){srcPop(anchor,d);return;}
if(d.wv){wavePop(anchor,d);return;}
if(d.p==='split'&&mk().mode===2){splitArm();return;}
if(d.L){listPop(anchor,d);return;}
lcdShow(lcdNm(d),'−/NO +/YES oder einen Regler der Platte drehen');
}
const HELP={k:'Regler: senkrecht ziehen = Wert, seitlich weg = fein; in der Gesamtansicht zoomt ein Tipp',key:'Taste: tippen schaltet',
lcd:'Anzeige: EDIT zeigt Seite und Wert; Tipp = Liste des Werts bzw. ▸ Zusatz-Ansicht',s:'SELECT 1–16: im Spielen Programme, in EDIT die Seiten',
'ain.1':'Pegel des Eingangs 1 (Instrument) · live, nicht gespeichert','ain.2':'Pegel des Eingangs 2 (Stimme) · live, nicht gespeichert',
'sq.sel':'SEQ EDIT: die 16 Regler setzen die Schritte der gewählten Sequenz (im Vocoder: Kanal-Pegel bzw. -Pan)',
'seq.rec':'REC: einen Regler drehen – seine Bewegung wird die Sequenz',tim:'TIMBRE SELECT: Timbre 1 ↔ 2 (bei DUAL und SPLIT)','fx.sel':'MOD/DELAY: wählt, was Regler 11 und 12 bearbeiten',
'vp.sel':'VIRTUAL PATCH: SELECT wählt den Patch, SOURCE und DESTINATION schalten ihn, Regler 13–16 = Intensität',edit:'EDIT: alle Werte auf den Seiten 01–26'};
function firstHint(id){const k=HELP[id]?id:/^k\d+$/.test(id)||KDEF[id]?'k':/^s\d+$/.test(id)?'s':id==='lcd'?'lcd':'key';hint('h:'+k,MK_LABEL,HELP[k]);}
let RQ=0;
function renderSoon(){if(!RQ)RQ=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{RQ=0;render();});}
function setLed(id,st){const L=PL.ui&&PL.ui.led[id];if(!L||L._s===st)return;L._s=st;L.className=(L.tagName==='I'&&L.classList.contains('led')?'led':'mkpl')+(st?' '+st:'');}
function paintKnob(id){
const K_=PL.ui.knob[id],d=defOf(id);let n=0;
if(d){const v=get(d),[lo,hi]=range(d);n=hi>lo?(d.log?Math.log(Math.max(lo,v)/lo)/Math.log(hi/lo):(v-lo)/(hi-lo)):0;}
const a=Math.round((-135+270*clamp(n,0,1))*10)/10;
if(K_.a!==a){K_.a=a;K_.cap.style.transform='rotate('+a+'deg)';}
const g=grau(id);if(K_.g!==g){K_.g=g;K_.n.classList.toggle('grau',g);}
}
const litOf=()=>(P.mode==='edit'?keyOfPage():P.mode==='spiel'?mkPanelProg('lit'):0);
const stepBase=(k,lit)=>(lit===k&&k?'on':'');
function leds(){
const vc=voc(),t=ct(),m=mk();
for(let k=0;k<8;k++)setLed('o1.led.'+k,t.o1.w===k?'on':'');
for(let k=0;k<3;k++)setLed('o2.led.'+k,!vc&&t.o2.w===k?'on':'');
setLed('o2.ms.0',!vc&&t.o2.mod&1?'on':'');setLed('o2.ms.1',!vc&&t.o2.mod&2?'on':'');
const fs=[0,1,2,-1,-2].indexOf(Math.round(m.voc.fs))-1;
for(let k=0;k<4;k++)setLed('f.led.'+k,(vc?fs===k:t.f.type===k)?'on':'');
setLed('a.gate',!vc&&t.a.gate?'on':'');setLed('a.dist',(vc?m.voc.dist:t.a.dist)?'on':'');
setLed('arp.on',m.arp.on?'on':'');setLed('arp.latch',m.arp.latch?'on':'');
const o=((S.tracks[P.i]&&S.tracks[P.i].kbOct)||4)-4,os=Math.abs(o)===1?'gn':Math.abs(o)===2?'red':Math.abs(o)>=3?'red bl':'';
setLed('oct.d',o<0?os:'');setLed('oct.u',o>0?os:'');
setLed('tim.led.0',!vc&&ti()===0?'on':'');setLed('tim.led.1',!vc&&ti()===1?'on':'');
setLed('seq.on',!vc&&t.seq.on?'on':'');setLed('seq.rec',P.rec?(P.rec.started?'on':'on bl'):'');
for(let k=0;k<4;k++){setLed('l1.led.'+k,t.l1.w===k?'on':'');setLed('l2.led.'+k,t.l2.w===k?'on':'');}
setLed('fx.sel',P.fxSel?'on':'');
const pp=t.p[P.patchSel];
for(let k=0;k<8;k++){setLed('vp.src.'+k,(vc?m.voc.msrc===k:pp.s===k)?'on':'');setLed('vp.dst.'+k,!vc&&pp.d===k?'on':'');}
for(let k=0;k<4;k++)setLed('vp.led.'+k,!vc&&P.patchSel===k?'on':'');
for(let k=0;k<3;k++)setLed('sq.led.'+k,(vc?P.chSel===k+1:P.seqSel===k+1)?'on':'');
const lit=P.run?0:litOf();for(let k=1;k<=16;k++)setLed('s'+k,P.run?(P.run===k?'on run':''):stepBase(k,lit));
for(const id of['o2.wave','o2.mod','a.gate','tim','seq.on','seq.rec','vp.sel','vp.d']){const b=PL.ui.key[id];if(b)b.classList.toggle('grau',grau(id));}
}
function paintOrig(){
const d=P.lastKnob;let on=false;
if(d&&d.p&&P.orig&&!VIEWP.test(d.p)&&d.p!=='bpm'&&d.p!=='vol'&&d.p!=='mw'&&!d.src){
const o=SQMK.mkPathGet(P.orig,tp(d.p));on=typeof o==='number'&&(d.wv?Math.floor(o/2)+1:o)===get(d);}
setLed('orig',on?'on':'');
}
function wiaPaint(){
const on=!!UI.wia;if(!on&&!P.wiaAny)return;P.wiaAny=on;const I0=on?SQMK.init():null;
for(const id in PL.ui.knob){const d=on?defOf(id):null,dif=!!(d&&d.p&&!VIEWP.test(d.p)&&d.p!=='bpm'&&d.p!=='vol'&&d.p!=='mw'&&SQMK.mkPathGet(I0,d.sst!=null?d.p:d.p)!==get(d)&&SQMK.mkPathGet(I0,d.p)!=null);
const n=PL.ui.knob[id].n;if(n._d!==dif){n._d=dif;n.classList.toggle('dif',dif);}}
for(let k=1;k<=16;k++){const b=PL.ui.key['s'+k],dif=on&&P.mode==='edit'&&pageDiff(k);if(b&&b._d!==dif){b._d=dif;b.classList.toggle('dif',dif);}}
}
function render(){
const U=P.el;if(!U||!U.fr||!U.fr.isConnected)return;
const s_=snd();if(!s_||!s_.p.mk)return;
einOn(true);
if(P.xv){if(P.xv.steps)stepsPaint(true);else if(P.xv.bands)bandsDraw();else if(P.xv.own)ownPaint();}
if(PL.ui){for(const id in PL.ui.knob)paintKnob(id);leds();paintOrig();wiaPaint();}
lcdPaint();spielPaint();navPaint();
}
const reduced=()=>{try{return matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;}catch(e){return false;}};
const wholeMode=()=>P.land||(P.el.fw>=700&&P.el.fh>=418);
function zOv(){const U=P.el;return wholeMode()?Math.min(U.fw,U.fh*1.95):Math.min(U.fw/.556,U.fh*1.95);}
function zHalt(n){const H=HALTE[n-1],U=P.el,fit=Math.min(U.fw/((H[3]-H[2])/100),U.fh*1.95/((H[5]-H[4])/100));return Math.max(zOv(),Math.min(1320,fit));}
function zMax(){let z=zOv();for(let n=1;n<=13;n++)z=Math.max(z,zHalt(n));return Math.round(Math.min(1320,z));}
function viewT(o){
o=o||P;const U=P.el;let z,cx,cy;
if(o.ansicht==='zoom'&&o.halt){const H=HALTE[o.halt-1];z=zHalt(o.halt);cx=(H[2]+H[3])/2;cy=(H[4]+H[5])/2;}
else{z=zOv();cy=50;cx=wholeMode()?50:o.haelfte==='rechts'?77.75:27.8;}
return{z,tx:U.fw/2-cx/100*z,ty:U.fh/2-cy/100*z/1.95};
}
function setHull(T,dx,anim){
const U=P.el,s=T.z/PL.zb,h=U.hull,tr='translate('+(T.tx+(dx||0)).toFixed(2)+'px,'+T.ty.toFixed(2)+'px) scale('+s.toFixed(5)+')';
if(anim&&!reduced()){h.style.willChange='transform';h.style.transition='transform 250ms ease-out';clearTimeout(U.animT);U.animT=setTimeout(()=>{h.style.transition='';h.style.willChange='';},270);}
else if(!U.swipe){h.style.transition='';if(!anim)h.style.willChange='';}
if(h._tr!==tr){h._tr=tr;h.style.transform=tr;}
}
function applyView(anim){const U=P.el;if(!U||!U.fw||!PL.el)return;U.T=viewT();setHull(U.T,0,anim);navPaint();}
function visRect(){const T=P.el.T;return[-T.tx*PW/T.z,-T.ty*PW/T.z,(P.el.fw-T.tx)*PW/T.z,(P.el.fh-T.ty)*PW/T.z];}
function zoomTo(n,anim){if(!n)return;P.ansicht='zoom';P.halt=n;P.letzterHalt=n;applyView(anim!==false);const H=HALTE[n-1];lcdShow('HALT',H[1]+' · '+n+'/13');haptic('tick');}
function toOverview(){if(P.ansicht==='zoom'&&P.halt){const H=HALTE[P.halt-1];P.haelfte=(H[2]+H[3])/2<55.6?'links':'rechts';}P.ansicht='ganz';applyView(true);lcdShow('GERÄT',wholeMode()?'die ganze Platte · Tipp auf eine Sektion = Zoom':'Hälfte '+P.haelfte.toUpperCase()+' · Wischen oder Karte = andere Hälfte');}
function step(dir){
if(P.tab!=='geraet'||P.view){tabTo('geraet');return;}
if(P.ansicht!=='zoom'){zoomTo(P.letzterHalt||1);return;}
const n=P.halt+dir;if(n<1||n>13){springBack();return;}zoomTo(n);
}
function springBack(){const U=P.el;if(!U||!U.T)return;setHull(U.T,0,true);}
function settledRect(pg){
const A=[];
try{const app=EL.app;if(app&&app.getAnimations)for(const a of app.getAnimations()){if(a.playState!=='running'||!a.effect)continue;
const t=a.effect.getComputedTiming();if(!isFinite(t.endTime))continue;A.push([a,a.currentTime]);a.currentTime=t.endTime;}}catch(e){}
const r=pg.getBoundingClientRect();
for(const[a,t]of A)try{a.currentTime=t;}catch(e){}
return r;
}
function panelLive(pg,i){return pg.isConnected&&R.editor==='synth'&&R.selT===i&&EL.synthSheet.classList.contains('open')&&S.tracks[i]&&S.tracks[i].sound.engine==='mk';}
function origSync(s_){const okey=s_.origin+'|'+s_.name;if(P.origKey!==okey||!P.orig){P.origKey=okey;const rec=originRec(s_);P.orig=deep(rec&&rec.p&&rec.p.mk?SQMK.normMk(rec.p.mk):s_.p.mk);}}
function build(pg,i){
if(P.i!==i){if(P.i>=0)leave();P.mode='spiel';P.seqSel=0;P.chSel=0;P.lastKnob=null;P.run=0;}
P.i=i;P.pg=pg;const s_=sndOf(i);if(!s_||!s_.p.mk)return;
const okey=s_.origin+'|'+s_.name;if(P.origKey&&P.origKey!==okey){P.mode='spiel';P.tim=1;}
if(!dualOn())P.tim=1;
if(P.view==='eigene'&&s_.p.mk.scale!==9)P.view=null;
if(P.view==='schritte'&&s_.p.mk.mode===3)P.view=null;
if(P.view==='baender'&&s_.p.mk.mode!==3)P.view=null;
origSync(s_);
P.tab=ED.tab==='spiel'?'spiel':'geraet';P.land=window.innerWidth>window.innerHeight;
pg.classList.add('mkpg');pg.innerHTML='';
const fr=el('div','mkfr'),hull=el('div','mkhull');fr.appendChild(hull);pg.appendChild(fr);
const U=P.el={fr,hull,pg,fw:0,fh:0,T:null};P.sp=null;P.xv=null;
const r=settledRect(fr);U.fw=Math.floor(r.width);U.fh=Math.floor(r.height);
frWire(fr);
if(!U.fw||!U.fh){requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(panelLive(pg,i)&&P.el===U)build(pg,i);});return;}
try{tickAhead(.3);}catch(e){}
const zb=zMax();if(!PL.el||PL.zb!==zb)buildPlate(zb);
hull.appendChild(PL.el);
if(P.view){hull.hidden=true;xvOpen();}
else if(P.tab==='spiel'){hull.hidden=true;buildSpiel();}
applyView(false);render();
if(typeof ResizeObserver==='undefined')requestAnimationFrame(coverSync);
try{const E_=AU.synth[i];if(E_)E_.mkScope(true);}catch(e){}
einOn(true);
if(typeof ResizeObserver!=='undefined'){U.ro=new ResizeObserver(()=>{
if(!fr.isConnected){U.ro.disconnect();return;}
coverSync();
const r2=fr.getBoundingClientRect(),w=Math.floor(r2.width),h=Math.floor(r2.height);if(!w||!h||(w===U.fw&&h===U.fh))return;
clearTimeout(U.roT);U.roT=setTimeout(()=>{if(!panelLive(pg,i)||P.el!==U)return;build(pg,i);},60);});U.ro.observe(fr);}
if(!P.seen){P.seen=true;lcdShow(MK_LABEL,wholeMode()?'die ganze Platte · drehen, tippen, eine Sektion antippen = Zoom':'eine Hälfte der Platte · Wischen = andere Hälfte · Sektion antippen = Zoom');}
}
const COVER=['gridArea','top','strip','modebar','carrier','kbArea'];
function coverSync(){
try{
const sh=EL.synthSheet;if(!sh||!sh.classList.contains('open')){coverOff();return;}
const a=sh.getBoundingClientRect();
for(const id of COVER){const n=document.getElementById(id);if(!n)continue;const b=n.getBoundingClientRect();
const cov=b.width>0&&b.height>0&&b.left>=a.left-.5&&b.right<=a.right+.5&&b.top>=a.top-.5&&b.bottom<=a.bottom+.5;
if(n.classList.contains('mkcov')!==cov)n.classList.toggle('mkcov',cov);}
}catch(e){}
}
function coverOff(){for(const id of COVER){const n=document.getElementById(id);if(n&&n.classList.contains('mkcov'))n.classList.remove('mkcov');}}
const PT=new Map();
function toPlate(fx,fy){const T=P.el.T;return[(fx-T.tx)*PW/T.z,(fy-T.ty)*PW/T.z];}
function frWire(fr){
guardTouch(fr);
fr.addEventListener('pointerdown',frDown);fr.addEventListener('pointermove',frMove);
fr.addEventListener('pointerup',ev=>frUp(ev,false));fr.addEventListener('pointercancel',ev=>frUp(ev,true));
fr.addEventListener('contextmenu',ev=>ev.preventDefault());
fr.addEventListener('click',()=>{});
}
function frDown(ev){
const U=P.el;if(ev.button>0||!U||!U.T||P.view||P.tab!=='geraet'||ev.target.closest('.mksp,.mkxv'))return;
ev.preventDefault();
const r=U.fr.getBoundingClientRect(),fx=ev.clientX-r.left,fy=ev.clientY-r.top,[x,y]=toPlate(fx,fy);
const e=hitAt(x,y,visRect());P.lastHit=(e?e.id:'frei')+(P.ansicht==='zoom'?'@'+P.halt:'');
const st={id:ev.pointerId,x0:ev.clientX,y0:ev.clientY,t0:performance.now(),e,px:x,py:y,kind:!e?'free':e.art==='k'?'knob':e.art==='lcd'?'lcd':'key'};
if(P.splitArm&&st.kind!=='lcd')splitOff();
for(const o of PT.values())if(e&&o.e===e)st.kind='ign';
try{U.fr.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId);}catch(_){}
PT.set(ev.pointerId,st);gestBegin(ev.pointerId,()=>{if(PT.get(ev.pointerId)===st)frEnd(st,null,true);});
if(st.kind==='knob')knobStart(st,ev);
else if(st.kind==='key'){const b=keyNode(e.id);if(b)b.classList.add('dn');if(e.id==='edit')P.editHeld++;}
else if(st.kind==='free'){RESTPTR.add(ev.pointerId);for(const o of PT.values())if(o.kind==='knob'&&o!==st){st.rest=1;break;}}
}
function frMove(ev){
const st=PT.get(ev.pointerId);if(!st)return;
const dx=ev.clientX-st.x0,dy=ev.clientY-st.y0;
if(st.kind==='knob'){knobMove(st,ev);return;}
if((st.kind==='key'||st.kind==='free'||st.kind==='lcd')&&!st.moved&&Math.hypot(dx,dy)>8){
st.moved=true;if(st.kind==='key'){const b=keyNode(st.e.id);if(b)b.classList.remove('dn');}
if(!st.rest&&Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)&&(P.ansicht==='zoom'||!wholeMode())){st.kind='swipe';RESTPTR.delete(st.id);P.el.swipe=st;P.el.hull.style.transition='';P.el.hull.style.willChange='transform';}
}
if(st.kind==='swipe'){const T=P.el.T,edge=P.ansicht==='zoom'?(dx>0&&P.halt<=1)||(dx<0&&P.halt>=13):(dx>0&&P.haelfte==='links')||(dx<0&&P.haelfte==='rechts');setHull(T,edge?dx*.35:dx,false);st.dx=dx;st.tl=performance.now();}
}
function frUp(ev,cancel){const st=PT.get(ev.pointerId);if(!st)return;frEnd(st,ev,cancel);}
function frEnd(st,ev,cancel){
PT.delete(st.id);if(GEST.has(st.id))gestEnd(st.id);RESTPTR.delete(st.id);
const dt=performance.now()-st.t0;
if(st.kind==='knob'){knobEnd(st,cancel);return;}
if(st.kind==='key'){const b=keyNode(st.e.id);if(b)b.classList.remove('dn');if(st.e.id==='edit')P.editHeld=Math.max(0,P.editHeld-1);if(!cancel&&!st.moved)press(st.e.id);return;}
if(st.kind==='lcd'){if(!cancel&&!st.moved){firstHint('lcd');lcdTap();}return;}
if(st.kind==='swipe'){
const U=P.el;U.swipe=null;const dx=st.dx||0,v=Math.abs(dx)/Math.max(1,dt);
if(!cancel&&(Math.abs(dx)>=40||v>=.3)){const dir=dx<0?1:-1;
if(P.ansicht==='zoom'){const n=P.halt+dir;if(n>=1&&n<=13){zoomTo(n);return;}}
else{const h=dir>0?'rechts':'links';if(h!==P.haelfte){P.haelfte=h;applyView(true);lcdShow('GERÄT','Hälfte '+h.toUpperCase());return;}}}
springBack();return;
}
if(st.kind==='free'&&!cancel&&!st.moved&&!st.rest&&dt<=300){
const n=haltAt(st.px,st.py);if(!n)return;
if(P.ansicht!=='zoom'||n!==P.halt)zoomTo(n);
}
}
function knobStart(st,ev){
const d=defOf(st.e.id);st.d=d;firstHint(st.e.id);if(!d)return;
st.before=isSnd(d)?soundSnapshot(P.i):null;st.v=get(d);st.v0=st.v;st.y=ev.clientY;st.st='down';st.path=tp(d.p);
histCurveStart();
for(const o of PT.values())if(o!==st&&o.kind==='knob'){o.multi=1;st.multi=1;}
if(d.ch!=null)P.ch0=null;
if(d.p==='voc.efs')P.efs0=st.v;
if(grau(st.e.id))lcdShow('VOCODER',GRAU[st.e.id]);else lcdOf(d);
}
function knobMove(st,ev){
const d=st.d;if(!d)return;
if(st.st==='down'){if(Math.abs(ev.clientY-st.y0)<CTL_SLOP&&Math.abs(ev.clientX-st.x0)<CTL_SLOP)return;st.st='drag';st.y=ev.clientY;}
if(d.src)return;
const[lo,hi]=range(d),n=hi-lo,list=(!!d.L||!!d.stp||d.p==='kbOct')&&n<=12,px=list?n*clamp(PX_FULL/Math.max(1,n),18,40):d.px||PX_FULL;
const f=ctlPrecision(Math.abs(ev.clientX-st.x0));
if(d.log){const r=Math.log(hi/lo);st.v=clamp(lo*Math.exp(clamp(Math.log(Math.max(lo,st.v)/lo)+(st.y-ev.clientY)/px*r*f,0,r)),lo,hi);}
else st.v=clamp(st.v+(st.y-ev.clientY)/px*n*f,lo,hi);
st.y=ev.clientY;
const nv=list||d.sst!=null?Math.round(st.v):st.v,before=Math.round(get(d));
if((list||d.sst!=null)&&nv===before)return;
if(P.rec)recTouch(d,st);
setv(d,nv,!list);
histCurve(get(d));
if(Math.round(get(d))!==before&&(list||d.sst!=null||(n>12&&d.L)||d.wv))haptic('tick');
P.lastKnob=d;paintKnob(st.e.id);lcdSoon(d);showVal(d);paintOrig();
}
let LCDQ=null,LCDR=0;
function lcdSoon(d){LCDQ=d;if(!LCDR)LCDR=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{LCDR=0;const q=LCDQ;LCDQ=null;if(q)lcdOf(q);});}
function lcdSoonDrop(){LCDQ=null;}
let LASTTAP=null;
function knobEnd(st,cancel){
const d=st.d;if(!d)return;lcdSoonDrop();
if(st.st==='drag'){
if(d.p==='voc.efs'){
setv(d,Math.round(st.v),false);P.lastKnob=d;const v=get(d);
if(v>=127&&!mk().voc.hold){P.efsPrev=P.efs0;holdToggle(st.before);return;}
if(v<127&&mk().voc.hold)mkSetP(P.i,'voc.hold',null);
commitSound(P.i,st.before,MK_LABEL+' E.F.SENSE');renderSoon();lcdOf(d);return;
}
if(!d.L&&d.p!=='bpm'&&d.p!=='vol'&&d.p!=='mw'&&!/^trim/.test(d.p))setv(d,d.step?Math.round(st.v/d.step)*d.step:Math.round(st.v),false);
else if(d.L&&d.nu)setv(d,Math.round(st.v),false);
if(recOwns(st)){recGestEnd(st);P.lastKnob=d;renderSoon();return;}
if(st.before!=null)commitSound(P.i,gestBefore(st),d.p==='vol'?'Lautstärke':MK_LABEL+' '+d.l);
P.lastKnob=d;renderSoon();lcdOf(d);return;
}
if(cancel)return;
if(P.ansicht!=='zoom'&&performance.now()-st.t0<=300){zoomTo(st.e.halt);return;}
const t=performance.now();
if(LASTTAP&&LASTTAP.id===st.e.id&&t-LASTTAP.t<DBL_MS+100){LASTTAP=null;knobDefault(d);return;}
LASTTAP={id:st.e.id,t};P.lastKnob=d;showVal(d);paintOrig();
}
function gestLeaf(path){return path==='mw'?['p','mw']:path==='vol'?['fx','vol']:['p','mk'].concat(String(path).split('.'));}
function gestBefore(st){
const run=[...PT.values()].filter(o=>o!==st&&o.kind==='knob'&&o.before!=null&&o.d&&isSnd(o.d)&&o.path);
if(!st.multi&&!run.length)return st.before;
let cur;try{cur=JSON.parse(soundSnapshot(P.i));}catch(_){return st.before;}
const sc=cur.$==='trk'?cur.sound:cur;
for(const g of[st].concat(run)){
if(!g.path||g.before==null)continue;
try{const B=JSON.parse(g.before),sb=B.$==='trk'?B.sound:B,ks=gestLeaf(g.path);let a=sb,b=sc;
for(let k=0;k<ks.length-1&&a&&b;k++){a=a[ks[k]];b=b[ks[k]];}
const last=ks[ks.length-1];if(a&&b&&typeof a==='object'&&typeof b==='object'&&last in a)b[last]=a[last];}catch(_){}
}
return JSON.stringify(cur);
}
function knobDefault(d){
let v=null;
if(d.sst!=null)v=0;else if(/^trim/.test(d.p))v=100;else if(d.p==='mw')v=0;
else if(d.p&&!VIEWP.test(d.p)&&d.p!=='bpm'&&d.p!=='vol'){const x=SQMK.mkPathGet(SQMK.init(),d.p);if(typeof x==='number')v=x;}
if(v==null)return;
pl1Rst(()=>{if(isSnd(d))soundEdit(P.i,MK_LABEL+' '+d.l+' Standard',()=>setv(d,v,false));else setv(d,v,false);});
haptic('snap');P.lastKnob=d;lcdOf(d);showVal(d);renderSoon();
}
function knobSim(path,vals){
const d=ELBY.has(path)?defOf(path):Object.values(KN).find(x=>x&&x.p===path)||STD.find(x=>x&&x.p===path);
if(!d)throw new Error('knobSim: kein Regler '+path);
const G={d,before:isSnd(d)?soundSnapshot(P.i):null,v0:get(d),v:get(d),st:'drag'};
for(const v of vals){recTouch(d,G);setv(d,v,true);G.v=v;P.lastKnob=d;showVal(d);}
if(recOwns(G)){setv(d,Math.round(G.v),false);recGestEnd(G);renderSoon();return'aufnahme';}
if(!d.L||d.nu)setv(d,Math.round(G.v),false);if(G.before!=null)commitSound(P.i,G.before,MK_LABEL+' '+d.l);renderSoon();return'geste';
}
function xvOpen(){
const U=P.el;if(!U||!U.fr)return;
const box=el('div','mkxv');U.fr.appendChild(box);U.xvBox=box;U.hull.hidden=true;
const land=U.fw>U.fh*1.4;
if(P.view==='schritte')buildSteps(box,U.fw,U.fh,land);
else if(P.view==='baender')buildBands(box,U.fw,U.fh,land);
else buildOwn(box,U.fw,U.fh,land);
}
function viewOpen(v){P.view=v;if(P.pg&&P.pg.isConnected)build(P.pg,P.i);}
function viewClose(){P.view=null;P.xv=null;if(P.pg&&P.pg.isConnected)build(P.pg,P.i);lcdShow('GERÄT','zurück zur Platte');}
function buildOwn(box,w,h,land){
const cols=land?12:6,rows=land?1:2,kw=clamp(Math.floor((w-(cols-1)*4)/cols),44,64),kh=clamp(Math.floor((h-50-(rows-1)*4)/rows),44,90);
const Wd=cols*kw+(cols-1)*4,Hd=46+rows*kh+(rows-1)*4;
const body=el('div','mkbody mkown');body.style.width=Wd+'px';body.style.height=Hd+'px';
const wrap=el('div','mkwrap');wrap.style.width=Wd+'px';wrap.style.height=Hd+'px';wrap.appendChild(body);box.appendChild(wrap);
const ui={body,wrap,own:true,knobs:[],E:{}};
const place=(node,x,y,ww,hh)=>{Object.assign(node.style,{left:x+'px',top:y+'px',width:ww+'px',height:hh+'px'});body.appendChild(node);ui.E[node.dataset.k]=[x,y,ww,hh];return node;};
const mkx=(k,cls,html)=>{const e=el('div','mkx '+cls,html);e.dataset.k=k;return e;};
ui.back=place(mkx('back','mkb mback','<span class="mkey">◂ GERÄT</span>'),0,0,96,44);ui.back.setAttribute('role','button');
ui.ttl=place(mkx('ttl','mprog','<span class="mpb"></span><span class="mpn"></span>'),100,0,Math.max(44,Wd-100),44);
ui.ttl.querySelector('.mpb').textContent='STIMMUNG · EIGENE';ui.ttl.querySelector('.mpn').textContent='Grundton '+keyName(mk().skey)+' · −100…+100 ct';
for(let k=0;k<12;k++){const e=place(mkx('u'+k,'mkxk',`<span class="lg">${esc(keyName((mk().skey+k)%12))}</span><div class="cap"><i></i></div>`),(k%cols)*(kw+4),46+Math.floor(k/cols)*(kh+4),kw,kh);
e.setAttribute('role','slider');ui.knobs.push(e);ownGesture(e,k);}
P.xv=ui;guardTouch(ui.back);onTap(ui.back,viewClose);
ownPaint();hint('own','EIGENE STIMMUNG','12 Regler: jede Stufe ab dem Grundton −100…+100 ct; ◂ GERÄT = zurück');
}
function ownPaint(){const U=P.xv;if(!U||!U.own)return;U.knobs.forEach((e,k)=>{const d=uscK(k),v=get(d),a=-135+270*(v+100)/200;e.querySelector('.cap').style.transform='rotate('+a.toFixed(1)+'deg)';e.setAttribute('aria-valuetext',fmtv(d,v)[1]);});}
function ownGesture(e,k){
guardTouch(e);let g=null;
e.addEventListener('pointerdown',ev=>{if(ev.button>0||g)return;ev.preventDefault();try{e.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId);}catch(_){}const d=uscK(k);
g={id:ev.pointerId,y:ev.clientY,x0:ev.clientX,y0:ev.clientY,v:get(d),d,before:soundSnapshot(P.i),drag:false};gestBegin(ev.pointerId,()=>{g=null;});lcdOf(d);});
e.addEventListener('pointermove',ev=>{if(!g||ev.pointerId!==g.id)return;if(!g.drag&&Math.abs(ev.clientY-g.y0)<CTL_SLOP)return;g.drag=true;
g.v=clamp(g.v+(g.y-ev.clientY)/PX_FULL*200*ctlPrecision(Math.abs(ev.clientX-g.x0)),-100,100);g.y=ev.clientY;setv(g.d,g.v,true);ownPaint();lcdOf(g.d);});
const end=ev=>{if(!g||ev.pointerId!==g.id)return;const G=g;g=null;if(GEST.has(ev.pointerId))gestEnd(ev.pointerId);
if(G.drag){setv(G.d,Math.round(G.v),false);commitSound(P.i,G.before,MK_LABEL+' EIGENE');ownPaint();}};
e.addEventListener('pointerup',end);e.addEventListener('pointercancel',end);
}
function ownInfo(){const U=P.xv;if(!U||!U.own||!U.body.isConnected)return null;const br=U.body.getBoundingClientRect();
return{w:br.width,h:br.height,els:Object.keys(U.E).map(k=>{const e=U.body.querySelector('.mkx[data-k="'+k+'"]'),r=e.getBoundingClientRect();return{k,x:r.left-br.left,y:r.top-br.top,w:r.width,h:r.height};})};}
const SPK={'oct.d':'OCT ▼','oct.u':'OCT ▲','bank.d':'BANK ▼','bank.u':'BANK ▲',tim:'TIMBRE','seq.on':'SEQ ON','seq.rec':'REC','arp.on':'ARP','arp.latch':'LATCH'};
function buildSpiel(){
const U=P.el,w=U.fw,h=U.fh,wide=w>=604,sp=el('div','mksp');U.fr.appendChild(sp);
const S_=P.sp={root:sp,keys:{},leds:{},steps:[],lcd:null,half:false};
const key=(id,row)=>{const b=el('button','mkspb'+(id==='tim'?' tim':''),`<i class="led"></i><span>${esc(SPK[id])}</span>`+(id==='tim'?'<i class="led l2"></i>':id==='arp.on'?'<i class="led tl"></i>':''));
b.dataset.k=id;row.appendChild(b);S_.keys[id]=b;onTap(b,()=>press(id));return b;};
const r1=el('div','mkspr');sp.appendChild(r1);
for(const id of['oct.d','oct.u','bank.d','bank.u'])key(id,r1);
if(wide)for(const id of['tim','seq.on','seq.rec','arp.on','arp.latch'])key(id,r1);
const lc=el('div','mkplcd mksplcd','<div class="ln"></div><div class="ln"></div>');r1.appendChild(lc);S_.lcd=lc;guardTouch(lc);onTap(lc,()=>lcdTap());
{const room=lc.clientWidth-13;let cw=.6;try{const c=PL.mc||(PL.mc=document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d'));c.font='600 100px '+getComputedStyle(lc).fontFamily;cw=c.measureText('MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM').width/1600;}catch(e){}
lc.style.fontSize=Math.max(7,Math.min(12,Math.floor(room/(16*cw)*2)/2))+'px';}
if(!wide&&w>=330){const r2=el('div','mkspr');sp.appendChild(r2);for(const id of['tim','seq.on','seq.rec','arp.on','arp.latch'])key(id,r2);}
const one=w>=704,half=!one&&(P.land||w<352),cols=one?16:P.land?8:half?4:8,rows=one||P.land?1:2,n=cols*rows;
S_.half=half;S_.n=n;
const grid=el('div','mkspg');grid.style.gridTemplateColumns='repeat('+cols+',minmax(0,1fr))';sp.appendChild(grid);S_.grid=grid;
for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const b=el('button','mksk','<b></b><i class="led"></i><span class="mkpr"></span>');grid.appendChild(b);S_.steps.push(b);onTap(b,()=>{const k=+b.dataset.n;if(k)press('s'+k);});}
if(half){const hp=el('button','mkhp','<span>1–'+n+'</span><span>'+(n+1)+'–16</span>');sp.appendChild(hp);S_.hp=hp;onTap(hp,()=>{P.spielHaelfte^=1;spielSteps();});
let sw=null;grid.addEventListener('pointerdown',ev=>{sw={x:ev.clientX,id:ev.pointerId};});
grid.addEventListener('pointerup',ev=>{if(sw&&sw.id===ev.pointerId&&Math.abs(ev.clientX-sw.x)>=40){P.spielHaelfte=ev.clientX<sw.x?1:0;spielSteps();}sw=null;});}
spielSteps();U.spKeep=S_;spielKb(U,sp);
}
function spielKb(U,sp){
if(P.land)requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(!sp.isConnected||sp.hidden)return;const fr=U.fr.getBoundingClientRect(),b=sp.getBoundingClientRect(),y=Math.round(b.bottom+4);
const st=(EL.kbArea||EL.app).style;st.setProperty('--mkkb-x',Math.round(fr.left)+'px');st.setProperty('--mkkb-y',y+'px');st.setProperty('--mkkb-w',Math.round(fr.width)+'px');st.setProperty('--mkkb-h',Math.max(60,Math.round(fr.bottom-y))+'px');

}
function spielSteps(){
const S_=P.sp;if(!S_)return;const off=S_.half?P.spielHaelfte*S_.n:0,vc=voc();
const cw=S_.cw||(S_.cw=S_.steps.length?S_.steps[0].clientWidth:0);
S_.steps.forEach((b,j)=>{const k=off+j+1;if(+b.dataset.n===k&&b._v===vc)return;b.dataset.n=k;b._v=vc;b.querySelector('b').textContent=k;const pr=b.querySelector('.mkpr'),tx=STEPP[k-1][vc?1:0],room=cw-7,wd=Math.max(...tx.split(' ').map(x=>measure(x,9,700)));pr.textContent=tx;pr.style.fontSize=wd<=room?'':Math.max(6.5,Math.floor(9*room/wd*2)/2)+'px';b.setAttribute('aria-label','SELECT '+k);});
if(S_.hp)S_.hp.classList.toggle('r',P.spielHaelfte===1);
spielPaint();
}
function spielPaint(){
const S_=P.sp;if(!S_||!S_.root.isConnected)return;
for(const id in S_.keys){const L=S_.keys[id].querySelector('.led'),src=PL.ui&&PL.ui.led[id];const st=id==='tim'?(!voc()&&ti()===0?'on':''):src?src._s||'':'';
if(L._s!==st){L._s=st;L.className='led'+(st?' '+st:'');}}
const t2=S_.keys.tim&&S_.keys.tim.querySelector('.l2');if(t2){const st=!voc()&&ti()===1?'on':'';if(t2._s!==st){t2._s=st;t2.className='led l2'+(st?' '+st:'');}}
const lit=P.run?0:litOf();for(const b of S_.steps){const k=+b.dataset.n,L=b.querySelector('.led'),st=P.run?(P.run===k?'on run':''):stepBase(k,lit);if(L._s!==st){L._s=st;L.className='led'+(st?' '+st:'');}}
if(S_.hp){const other=P.run&&(P.spielHaelfte?P.run<=S_.n:P.run>S_.n);S_.hp.classList.toggle('dot',!!other);}
}
let NAV=null;
function navEl(){
const n=el('div','mknav'),a=el('button','mkar','◀'),m=el('button','mkmap','<canvas width="64" height="33"></canvas><i class="mkmr"></i>'),b=el('button','mkar','▶');

onTap(m,(_,ev)=>{
if(P.tab!=='geraet'||P.view){tabTo('geraet');return;}
if(P.ansicht==='zoom'){toOverview();return;}
if(!wholeMode()&&ev){const r=m.getBoundingClientRect();P.haelfte=ev.clientX-r.left<r.width*.556?'links':'rechts';applyView(true);lcdShow('GERÄT','Hälfte '+P.haelfte.toUpperCase());}
});
NAV={n,a,b,m,cv:m.querySelector('canvas'),r:m.querySelector('.mkmr')};mapDraw();return n;
}
function mapDraw(){
const cv=NAV&&NAV.cv;if(!cv)return;const dpr=Math.min(3,window.devicePixelRatio||1);cv.width=Math.round(64*dpr);cv.height=Math.round(33*dpr);
const c=cv.getContext('2d');if(!c)return;const sx=64*dpr/PW,sy=33*dpr/PH;
c.fillStyle='#a9b8c6';c.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);c.strokeStyle='#2a2f35';c.lineWidth=Math.max(.6,.5*dpr);
for(const q of SEC)c.strokeRect(q[4]*XU*sx,q[6]*YU*sy,(q[5]-q[4])*XU*sx,(q[7]-q[6])*YU*sy);
c.fillStyle='#16191c';
for(const e of ELS){if(e.art==='k'){c.beginPath();c.arc(e.X*sx,e.Y*sy,1.1*dpr,0,6.3);c.fill();}else if(e.art==='b'||e.art==='bl'||e.art==='s')c.fillRect(e.x0*sx,e.y0*sy,Math.max(1,e.w*sx),Math.max(1,e.h*sy));else if(e.art==='lcd'){c.fillStyle='#9bbf4a';c.fillRect(e.x0*sx,e.y0*sy,e.w*sx,e.h*sy);c.fillStyle='#16191c';}}
}
function navPaint(){

let r=[0,0,PW,PH];if(plate)r=visRect();
const x0=clamp(r[0]/PW,0,1),x1=clamp(r[2]/PW,0,1),y0=clamp(r[1]/PH,0,1),y1=clamp(r[3]/PH,0,1);
const tr='translate('+(x0*64).toFixed(1)+'px,'+(y0*33).toFixed(1)+'px) scale('+Math.max(.02,x1-x0).toFixed(3)+','+Math.max(.02,y1-y0).toFixed(3)+')';
if(NAV.r._t!==tr){NAV.r._t=tr;NAV.r.style.transform=tr;}
NAV.r.hidden=!plate;
}
function tabTo(t){if(tabFast(t))return;ED.tab=t;FXF.big=null;safeRebuild(buildSynthEd);}
function tabFast(t){
const a=ED.tab,ok=x=>x==='geraet'||x==='spiel',pg=P.pg;
if(a===t||!ok(a)||!ok(t)||!pg||P.i<0||!panelLive(pg,P.i)||FXF.big)return false;
const U=P.el;if(!U||!U.fr||!U.fr.isConnected||!U.T||P.view||U.hull.hidden)return false;
ED.tab=t;P.tab=t;
for(const b of EL.synthSheet.querySelectorAll('.sh-tabs .tab'))b.classList.toggle('on',b.dataset.tab===t);
if(t==='spiel'){U.hull._tr='translate(-100000px,0px)';U.hull.style.transition='';U.hull.style.transform=U.hull._tr;const K_=U.spKeep;if(K_&&K_.root.isConnected){K_.root.hidden=false;P.sp=K_;spielSteps();spielKb(U,K_.root);}else buildSpiel();}
else{if(P.sp){P.sp.root.hidden=true;P.sp=null;}if(EL.app.classList.contains('mkqkb')){releaseInputs(r=>r==='play'||r==='pad');EL.app.classList.remove('mkqkb');UI.kbRelayout=true;}applyView(false);}
render();return true;
}

'<button class="rb" data-a="init"><b>Init</b><small>neutraler Startklang</small></button><button class="rb mkqhear" data-a="hear"><b>▶ Hören</b><small>halten = spielen</small></button>'+

else if(a==='wia')wiaToggle();else if(a==='init')initSound(i);else if(a==='tools')openEdMore(anchor,i);else if(a==='k1m')openK1Mods(anchor,i);},'button[data-a]');
},{name:'mkqmore'});
}
function leave(){
coverOff();
einOn(false);P.tim=1;P.splitArm=false;UI.mkSplitArm=null;UI.mkSplitOff=null;P.view=null;P.xv=null;P.ch0=null;P.editHeld=0;
if(P.rec)recCancel('abgebrochen (anderer Sound oder Editor zu)');
try{const E=AU.synth[P.i];if(E&&E.mkScope)E.mkScope(false);}catch(e){}
}
const REP={n:-1,q:[]};
function runStep(tA){
if(voc())return 0;const E=AU.synth[P.i];if(!E||!E.mks||!E.mks.seq)return 0;
if(E.mksN!==REP.n){REP.n=E.mksN;const sq=E.mks.seq[ti()];REP.q.push({t:E.mks.t,sq});if(REP.q.length>6)REP.q.shift();}
let r=null;for(const x of REP.q)if(x.t<=tA+1e-4)r=x;if(!r)r=REP.q[0];if(!r||!r.sq)return 0;
const sq=r.sq;if(!(sq.on||P.rec)||!sq.run)return 0;
let s=sq.step;if(sq.rem!=null&&sq.nx&&tA>=r.t+sq.rem*60/S.bpm)s=sq.nx;
return s;
}
function frame(now,bAud){
const U=P.el;if(!U||!U.fr||!U.fr.isConnected||P.i<0)return;
const s_=sndOf(P.i);if(!s_||s_.engine!=='mk'||!s_.p.mk)return;
if(typeof pl1Ll==='function'&&!pl1Ll()){ma1Frame();return;}
const tA=now-(AU.outLat||0),ph=R.playing?bAud:tA*S.bpm/60,led=((ph%1)+1)%1<.5;
setLed('arp.led',led?'on':'');
if(P.sp&&P.sp.keys['arp.on']){const L=P.sp.keys['arp.on'].querySelector('.tl');if(L&&L._on!==led){L._on=led;L.classList.toggle('on',led);}}
const want=!voc()&&(ct().seq.on||!!P.rec);try{const E=AU.synth[P.i];if(E&&want&&!E.mksOn)E.mkScope(true);}catch(e){}
const k=want?runStep(tA):0;P.runTA=tA;
if(k!==P.run){P.run=k;P.runN=(P.runN||0)+1;const lit=k?0:litOf();if(PL.ui)for(let j=1;j<=16;j++)setLed('s'+j,k?(k===j?'on run':''):stepBase(j,lit));spielPaint();}
ma1Frame();
}
const MA1_KN={1:'o2.tune',2:'o1.c1',3:'mix.n',4:'f.cut',5:'a.lvl',6:'a.pan',7:'k10'};
const MA1_SPAN=[24,63,127,127,127,127,63,127];
const MA1_SC=['fenv','menv','lfo1','lfo2','vel','key','rnd','mw'],MA1_BIP=[0,0,1,1,0,1,1,0];
const MA1={last:0,ids:new Set()};
function ma1N(d,v){const[lo,hi]=range(d);return clamp(hi>lo?(d.log?Math.log(Math.max(lo,v)/lo)/Math.log(hi/lo):(v-lo)/(hi-lo)):0,0,1);}
function ma1Arc(n0,n1){
const R_=17.6,a0=(-135+270*Math.min(n0,n1))*Math.PI/180,a1=(-135+270*Math.max(n0,n1))*Math.PI/180,f=x=>x.toFixed(2);
if(a1-a0<.02)return'';
return'M'+f(16.5+R_*Math.sin(a0))+' '+f(16.5-R_*Math.cos(a0))+'A'+R_+' '+R_+' 0 '+(a1-a0>Math.PI?1:0)+' 1 '+f(16.5+R_*Math.sin(a1))+' '+f(16.5-R_*Math.cos(a1));
}
function ma1Off(id){const K_=PL.ui&&PL.ui.knob[id];if(K_&&K_.ma1){K_.ma1.svg.remove();K_.ma1=null;}MA1.ids.delete(id);}
function ma1Frame(){
const now=performance.now();if(now-MA1.last<33)return;MA1.last=now;
const want=new Map();
if(PL.ui&&P.tab!=='spiel'&&!P.view&&!voc()){
const es=typeof v6aSnd==='function'?v6aSnd(P.i):null,T=es&&es.p&&es.p.mk&&es.p.mk.t?es.p.mk.t[ti()]||ct():ct();
T.p.forEach(c=>{const d=Math.round(c.d),id=MA1_KN[d];if(!c.i||!id||(d===7&&(T.l2.sync||P.seqSel)))return;if(!want.has(id))want.set(id,[]);want.get(id).push(c);});
}
for(const id of[...MA1.ids])if(!want.has(id)||!PL.ui.knob[id]||!PL.ui.knob[id].ma1)ma1Off(id);
if(!want.size)return;
const E=AU.synth[P.i];if(E&&E.mkScope&&!E.mksOn)E.mkScope(true);
const fresh=E&&E.mks&&AU.ctx&&AU.ctx.currentTime-E.mks.t<.5,t2=ti()===1;
let v=null;if(fresh)for(const x of E.mks.voices||[]){if((x.tb===2)!==t2)continue;if(!v||(x.gate&&!v.gate)||(!!x.gate===!!v.gate&&x.t<v.t))v=x;}
const mw=clamp(mkWheelVal(P.i,'mod'),0,1),bd=clamp(mkWheelVal(P.i,'bend'),-1,1);
for(const[id,rs]of want){
const K_=PL.ui.knob[id],d=defOf(id);if(!K_||!d)continue;
let o=K_.ma1;
if(!o){
K_.n.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<svg class="ma1mk" viewBox="0 0 33 33" aria-hidden="true"><path class="r"/><g class="dg"><circle class="h" cx="16.5" cy="-1.1" r="3.4"/><circle class="d" cx="16.5" cy="-1.1" r="2"/></g></svg>');
const svg=K_.n.lastChild;o=K_.ma1={svg,r:svg.querySelector('.r'),g:svg.querySelector('.dg'),rk:'',a:null,c:''};MA1.ids.add(id);
}
const base=get(d);let lo=0,hi=0,x=0,main=rs[0];
for(const c of rs){
const s=Math.round(c.s),a=c.i/63*MA1_SPAN[Math.round(c.d)];
if(MA1_BIP[s]){lo-=Math.abs(a);hi+=Math.abs(a);}else{lo+=Math.min(0,a);hi+=Math.max(0,a);}
if(Math.abs(c.i)>Math.abs(main.i))main=c;
if(v)x+=a*(s===0?v.eg1:s===1?v.eg2:s===2?v.lfo1:s===3?v.lfo2:s===4?(v.vel!=null?v.vel:.8):s===5?((v.pitch||60)-60)/60:s===6?bd:mw)||0;
}
const col=MSRC[MA1_SC[Math.round(main.s)]].c,rk=ma1Arc(ma1N(d,base+lo),ma1N(d,base+hi));
if(o.c!==col){o.c=col;o.svg.style.setProperty('--mc',col);}
if(o.rk!==rk){o.rk=rk;o.r.setAttribute('d',rk);}
const a=v?Math.round((-135+270*ma1N(d,base+x))*10)/10:null;
if(o.a!==a){o.a=a;if(a==null)o.g.setAttribute('visibility','hidden');else{o.g.setAttribute('visibility','visible');o.g.setAttribute('transform','rotate('+a+' 16.5 16.5)');}}
}
}
const ZCACHE={k:null,Z:null};
function panelInfo(){
const U=P.el;if(!U||!U.fr||!U.fr.isConnected||!U.T)return null;
const fr=U.fr.getBoundingClientRect(),T=U.T,s=T.z/PW,vis=visRect(),plate=P.tab==='geraet'&&!P.view;
const zk=plate?vis.map(v=>v.toFixed(2)).join(','):'';
let Z=plate&&ZCACHE.k===zk?ZCACHE.Z:null;
if(!Z){Z=new Map();if(plate){const cs=5;for(let y=cs/2;y<PH;y+=cs)for(let x=cs/2;x<PW;x+=cs){if(x<vis[0]||x>vis[2]||y<vis[1]||y>vis[3])continue;const e=hitAt(x,y,vis);if(!e)continue;
const z=Z.get(e.id)||{x0:1e9,y0:1e9,x1:-1e9,y1:-1e9};z.x0=Math.min(z.x0,x-cs/2);z.y0=Math.min(z.y0,y-cs/2);z.x1=Math.max(z.x1,x+cs/2);z.y1=Math.max(z.y1,y+cs/2);Z.set(e.id,z);}ZCACHE.k=zk;ZCACHE.Z=Z;}}
const scr=(x,y,w,h)=>({x:+(fr.left+T.tx+x*s).toFixed(2),y:+(fr.top+T.ty+y*s).toFixed(2),w:+(w*s).toFixed(2),h:+(h*s).toFixed(2)});
const pg=curPage(),[a,b]=lcdLines();
return{fmt:window.innerWidth+'x'+window.innerHeight+(P.land?' quer':' hoch'),rahmen:{x:fr.left,y:fr.top,w:fr.width,h:fr.height},z:+T.z.toFixed(2),zOv:+zOv().toFixed(2),zb:PL.zb,plateMs:+(PL.ms||0).toFixed(1),
ansicht:P.view?'zusatz':P.tab==='spiel'?'spiel':P.ansicht,haelfte:wholeMode()?undefined:P.haelfte,halt:P.ansicht==='zoom'?P.halt:undefined,view:P.view||undefined,
halte:HALTE.map(H=>({nr:H[0],station:H[1],x:H[2],y:H[4],w:H[3]-H[2],h:H[5]-H[4],z:+zHalt(H[0]).toFixed(2)})),
modus:P.mode,seite:pg?pg[0]+' '+pg[1]:'',cursor:P.cur,timbre:ti()+1,fxSel:P.fxSel,patchSel:P.patchSel,seqSel:P.seqSel,chSel:P.chSel,lauf:P.run||undefined,runN:P.runN||0,lcd:[a,b],hit:P.lastHit,rec:P.rec?{k:P.rec.k,started:P.rec.started}:undefined,
sections:SEC.map(q=>({id:q[0],title:q[1].replace('|',''),x0:q[4],x1:q[5],y0:q[6],y1:q[7]})),
elements:ELS.map(e=>{const d=e.act?defOf(e.id):null,z=Z.get(e.id),L=PL.ui&&(PL.ui.led[e.id]);
return{id:e.id,art:e.art,x:e.x,y:e.y,w:+(e.w/XU).toFixed(3),h:+(e.h/YU).toFixed(3),halt:e.halt,px:plate?scr(e.x0,e.y0,e.w,e.h):undefined,zone:z?scr(z.x0,z.y0,z.x1-z.x0,z.y1-z.y0):undefined,
bind:e.act?bindOf(e.id)||undefined:undefined,val:d?get(d):undefined,grau:grau(e.id),led:L?L._s||'':undefined};}),
steps:P.xv&&P.xv.steps?stepsInfo():undefined,spiel:P.sp?spielInfo():undefined};
}
function spielInfo(){const S_=P.sp,r=el=>{const q=el.getBoundingClientRect();return{x:q.left,y:q.top,w:q.width,h:q.height};};
return{keys:Object.keys(S_.keys).map(k=>Object.assign({k},r(S_.keys[k]))),steps:S_.steps.map(b=>Object.assign({n:+b.dataset.n,led:b.querySelector('.led')._s||''},r(b))),lcd:r(S_.lcd),half:S_.half,haelfte:P.spielHaelfte};}
function view(o){
o=o||{};
if(o.modus)P.mode=o.modus;if(o.seite!=null){const L=pages(),k=typeof o.seite==='number'?o.seite:L.findIndex(p=>p[0]===o.seite);if(k>=0)goPage(k,o.cursor||0);}
if(o.haelfte)P.haelfte=o.haelfte;
if(o.ansicht==='spiel'||o.ansicht==='fx'){if(ED.tab!==o.ansicht)tabTo(o.ansicht);return;}
if(o.ansicht==='ganz'||o.ansicht==='zoom'){if(o.ansicht==='zoom'){P.ansicht='zoom';P.halt=o.halt||P.letzterHalt||1;P.letzterHalt=P.halt;}else P.ansicht='ganz';
