function bandsLive(b){const U=P.xv;if(!U||!U.bands||!U.body.isConnected)return;P.bLv=b;if(!P.bRaf)P.bRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{P.bRaf=0;bandsDraw();});}
function bandsDraw(){

else{const m=iy+ih/2,f=clamp(V[b]/63,-1,1);x.fillStyle='#3a3b3f';x.fillRect(cx+4,m-.5,w-8,1);x.fillStyle='#ff7a3d';const px=cx+w/2+f*(w/2-5);x.fillRect(Math.min(cx+w/2,px),iy+4,Math.max(2,Math.abs(px-cx-w/2)),ih-8);}
if(L){const db_=L[b]>0?20*Math.log10(L[b]):-99,f=clamp((db_+60)/60,0,1);x.fillStyle='#53d27a';x.fillRect(cx+w-6,iy+ih*(1-f),3,ih*f);}
}
}

function sqVals(){return ct().seq.q[P.sq].v;}
function sqTgt(){return Math.round(ct().seq.q[P.sq].k);}
function buildSteps(box,w,h,land){

}
function colRect(g,c){const r=Math.floor(c/g.cols),k=c%g.cols;return[k*(g.cw+g.gap),r*(g.ch+4),g.cw,g.ch];}
function colAt(g,x,y){const r=g.rows>1&&y>=g.ch+2?1:0;let k=Math.floor((x+g.gap/2)/(g.cw+g.gap));k=clamp(k,0,g.cols-1);return r*g.cols+k;}
function valAt(g,c,y,R){const[,cy,,chh]=colRect(g,c),mid=cy+chh/2,half=chh/2-6;return clamp(Math.round((mid-y)/half*R),-R,R);}
function stepsCur(){const E=AU.synth[P.i],m=E&&E.mks&&E.mks.seq?E.mks.seq[ti()]:null;return m&&(m.on||m.rec)&&m.run!==0?m.step:0;}
function stepsPaint(force){
const U=P.xv;if(!U||!U.steps||!U.cv.isConnected)return;
const S_=ct().seq,v=sqVals(),tg=sqTgt(),R=tg?sqR(tg):63,cur=stepsCur(),on=S_.on||P.rec,g=U.geo,sh_=sqShadow(P.sq);
const key=JSON.stringify(v)+'|'+tg+'|'+S_.last+'|'+cur+'|'+on+'|'+P.sq+'|'+(P.rec?1:0)+'|'+sh_;
if(!force&&key===SQV.key)return;SQV.key=key;
U.sel.forEach((b,k)=>{b.classList.toggle('on',k===P.sq);b.querySelector('.mkey').textContent='SEQ '+(k+1)+(sqShadow(k)>=0?'*':'');});
U.rec.classList.toggle('on',!!P.rec);U.rec.classList.toggle('blink',!!P.rec);
if(U.ttl){const wide=U.ttl.clientWidth>=150;U.ttl.querySelector('.mpb').textContent='SEQ '+(P.sq+1)+(wide?' · ':' ')+(S_.on?'AN':'AUS')+(tg&&wide?' · ±'+R:'')+(sh_>=0?' *':'');U.ttl.querySelector('.mpn').textContent=tg?(wide?L_.sqtg[0][tg]:L_.sqsh[0][tg]):wide?'ZIEL wählen':'ZIEL ?';}
U.cv.setAttribute('aria-label','Schritte SEQ '+(P.sq+1)+': '+v.slice(0,S_.last).map(x=>clamp(x,-R,R)).join(', '));
const c=U.cv.getContext('2d');if(!c)return;const d=U.dpr;c.setTransform(d,0,0,d,0,0);c.clearRect(0,0,g.W,U.cv.height/d);
for(let s=0;s<16;s++){
const[x,y,w,h]=colRect(g,s),act=s<S_.last,val=clamp(v[s],-R,R),mid=y+h/2,half=h/2-6,bh=val/R*half;
c.fillStyle=s+1===cur&&on?'#3a2a1a':act?'#111214':'#0b0b0c';c.fillRect(x,y,w,h);
c.strokeStyle=g.gap?'#2b2c30':'#1d1e21';c.lineWidth=1;c.strokeRect(x+.5,y+.5,w-1,h-1);
c.fillStyle='#3a3b3f';c.fillRect(x+2,mid-.5,w-4,1);
c.fillStyle=!act?'#4a3a2c':s+1===cur&&on?'#ffd27a':tg?'#ff8a3c':'#6a6862';
if(bh>=0)c.fillRect(x+5,mid-bh,w-10,Math.max(1,bh));else c.fillRect(x+5,mid,w-10,-bh);
c.fillStyle=act?'#d6d3cc':'#6f6c66';c.font='700 9px system-ui, sans-serif';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='alphabetic';
c.fillText(String(s+1),x+w/2,y+h-2);
c.textBaseline='top';c.fillText(val>0?'+'+val:String(val),x+w/2,y+2);
}
}
function stepsLoop(){
cancelAnimationFrame(SQV.raf);
const tick=t=>{const U=P.xv;if(!U||!U.steps||!U.cv.isConnected){SQV.raf=0;return;}
if(!document.hidden&&t-SQV.last>=33){SQV.last=t;const E=AU.synth[P.i];if(E&&!E.mksOn)E.mkScope(true);stepsPaint(false);}
SQV.raf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);};
SQV.raf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
function stepSet(c,v){const i=P.i,tg=sqTgt(),R=tg?sqR(tg):63;mkSetP(i,'t.'+ti()+'.seq.q.'+P.sq+'.v.'+c,clamp(Math.round(v),-R,R),false);}
function stepsGesture(cv){
guardTouch(cv);let g=null;
const pos=ev=>{const r=cv.getBoundingClientRect(),U=P.xv,s=r.width/U.geo.W;return[(ev.clientX-r.left)/s,(ev.clientY-r.top)/s];};
on(cv,'pointerdown',ev=>{
if(ev.button>0||g)return;
ev.preventDefault();ev.stopPropagation();try{cv.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId);}catch(_){}
const[x,y]=pos(ev),G_=P.xv.geo,c=colAt(G_,x,y);
g={id:ev.pointerId,x0:x,y0:y,c,st:'down',v0:sqVals()[c],before:soundSnapshot(P.i),last:c};
gestBegin(ev.pointerId,()=>end(null));
});
on(cv,'pointermove',ev=>{
if(!g||ev.pointerId!==g.id)return;
const[x,y]=pos(ev),G_=P.xv.geo,tg=sqTgt(),R=tg?sqR(tg):63;
if(g.st==='down'){if(Math.abs(x-g.x0)>8){g.st='paint';stepSet(g.c,valAt(G_,g.c,g.y0,R));}else if(Math.abs(y-g.y0)>=CTL_SLOP)g.st='drag';else return;}
if(g.st==='paint'){
const c=colAt(G_,x,y),a=Math.min(c,g.last),b=Math.max(c,g.last),v=valAt(G_,c,y,R);
for(let k=a;k<=b;k++)if(Math.floor(k/G_.cols)===Math.floor(c/G_.cols)){if(sqVals()[k]!==v)stepSet(k,v);}
g.last=c;
}else{
const v=clamp(Math.round(g.v0+(g.y0-y)/G_.ch*2*R),-R,R);if(v!==sqVals()[g.c]){stepSet(g.c,v);haptic('tick');}
}
stepsPaint(true);stepLcd(g.st==='paint'?g.last:g.c);
});
const end=ev=>{
if(!g)return;const G=g;g=null;
if(ev){try{cv.releasePointerCapture(ev.pointerId);}catch(_){}if(GEST.has(ev.pointerId))gestEnd(ev.pointerId);}
if(G.st==='down'&&ev){
const G_=P.xv.geo,tg=sqTgt(),R=tg?sqR(tg):63,[,cy,,chh]=colRect(G_,G.c),mid=cy+chh/2,half=chh/2-6,bar=mid-clamp(sqVals()[G.c],-R,R)/R*half;
stepSet(G.c,clamp(sqVals()[G.c]+(G.y0<bar?1:-1),-R,R));haptic('tick');
}
commitSound(P.i,G.before,MK_LABEL+' SEQ '+(P.sq+1)+' Schritte');renderSoon();stepsPaint(true);stepLcd(G.c);
};
on(cv,'pointerup',ev=>{if(g&&ev.pointerId===g.id)end(ev);});
on(cv,'pointercancel',ev=>{if(g&&ev.pointerId===g.id)end(null);});
}
function stepLcd(c){const tg=sqTgt(),R=tg?sqR(tg):63,v=clamp(sqVals()[c],-R,R);lcdShow(tLab()+'SEQ '+(P.sq+1)+' · SCHRITT '+(c+1),(v>0?'+':'')+v+(tg?' '+L_.sqtg[0][tg]+' (±'+R+')':' · ZIEL wählen (Seite '+(19+P.sq)+' SEQ '+(P.sq+1)+')'));dispShow(z3(v));}
function stepsInfo(){const U=P.xv;if(!U||!U.steps||!U.body.isConnected)return null;const br=U.body.getBoundingClientRect(),r=U.cv.getBoundingClientRect(),s=r.width/U.geo.W;
return{w:br.width,h:br.height,geo:U.geo,cv:{x:r.left,y:r.top,w:r.width,h:r.height,s},cols:[...Array(16).keys()].map(c=>{const q=colRect(U.geo,c);return{c,x:r.left+q[0]*s,y:r.top+q[1]*s,w:q[2]*s,h:q[3]*s};}),
keys:['back','s0','s1','s2','rec'].map(k=>{const e=U.body.querySelector('.mkx[data-k="'+k+'"]'),q=e.getBoundingClientRect();return{k,x:q.left,y:q.top,w:q.width,h:q.height};}),cur:stepsCur(),sq:P.sq};}
const recTgOf=d=>{if(!d||!d.p||!d.p.startsWith('t.0.'))return 0;const k=W().SQT.indexOf(d.p.slice(4));return k>0&&k!==2?k:0;};
function recArm(k){
if(P.rec){recCancel('abgebrochen');stepsPaint(true);return;}
P.rec={k,ti:ti(),started:false,G:null,done:null};P.sq=k;
haptic('tick');renderSoon();lcdShow('● REC · SEQ '+(k+1),'jetzt einen Regler der Platte drehen: er wird das ZIEL; aufgenommen wird ab dem laufenden Schritt bis LAST STEP · REC noch einmal = abbrechen');
}
function recTouch(d,G){
const r=P.rec;if(!r||r.started)return;
const tg=d.sst==null?recTgOf(d):0;
if(!tg){if(!r.warned){r.warned=1;lcdShow('● REC',d.l+' nimmt nicht auf – ein Klang-Regler (Oszillator, Mixer, Filter, Amp, Hüllkurven, LFO, PATCH)');}return;}
const E=AU.synth[P.i];if(!E||!E.node){recCancel('kein Studio-Klang');return;}
Object.assign(r,{started:true,d,tg,base:G.v0,before:G.before,G,ti:ti()});
try{E.node.port.postMessage({mkRec:{ti:r.ti,k:r.k,tg,base:G.v0}});}catch(e){logErr(e);}
render();
}
const recOwns=G=>!!(P.rec&&P.rec.G===G);
function recGestEnd(G){const r=P.rec;r.G=null;if(r.done)recFinish();}
function recDone(E,m){
const r=P.rec;if(!r||!r.started||AU.synth[P.i]!==E||m.ti!==r.ti||m.k!==r.k)return;
r.done=m;if(!r.G)recFinish();
}
function recFinish(){
const r=P.rec;P.rec=null;const i=P.i,cur=sndOf(i);if(!cur||!cur.p.mk)return;
const s_=deep(cur),pm=s_.p.mk,path=W().SQT[r.tg],m=r.done,b=JSON.parse(r.before),bs=b.sound||b;
for(const k of[0,1]){const o=bs.p&&bs.p.mk?SQMK.mkPathGet(bs.p.mk,'t.'+k+'.'+path):undefined;if(typeof o==='number')SQMK.mkPathSet(pm,'t.'+k+'.'+path,o);}
const q=pm.t[r.ti].seq.q[r.k];q.k=r.tg;q.v=m.v.slice(0,16);pm.t[r.ti].seq.on=1;s_.p.mk=SQMK.normMk(pm);UI.ab=null;
applySound(i,s_);commitSound(i,r.before,MK_LABEL+' Bewegung aufnehmen');
P.sq=r.k;render();lcdShow('● AUFN. · SEQ '+(r.k+1),L_.sqtg[0][r.tg]+' · Schritte '+m.from+'–'+m.to+' aufgenommen · SEQ an · ↶ = zurück');
}
function recCancel(why){
const r=P.rec;if(!r)return;P.rec=null;
if(r.started){try{const E=AU.synth[P.i];if(E&&E.node)E.node.port.postMessage({mkRec:{ti:r.ti,cancel:1}});}catch(e){}if(!r.G&&r.before)commitSound(P.i,r.before,MK_LABEL+' '+r.d.l);}
renderSoon();if(why)lcdShow('● REC',why);
}
const seqT=i=>tDst(i);
function seqInit(i,k){const t=seqT(i);timApply(i,'Sequenz neu',pm=>{const I0=SQMK.init().t[0].seq;if(k==='all')pm.t[t].seq=I0;else pm.t[t].seq.q[k]=I0.q[0];});lcdShow('SEQ · NEU',(k==='all'?'alle drei Sequenzen':'SEQ '+(k+1))+' auf INIT · ↶ = zurück');}
function seqSwap(i,a,b){const t=seqT(i);timApply(i,'Sequenzen tauschen',pm=>{const q=pm.t[t].seq.q,x=q[a];q[a]=q[b];q[b]=x;});lcdShow('SEQ · TAUSCHEN','SEQ '+(a+1)+' ⇄ SEQ '+(b+1)+' · ↶ = zurück');}
function seqCopy(i,src,srcT,srcK,dstK){
const t=seqT(i),sp=src&&src.p&&src.p.mk?SQMK.normMk(src.p.mk):null;if(!sp)return false;const S0=sp.t[srcT?1:0].seq;
timApply(i,srcK==='all'?'Sequenzen kopieren':'Sequenz kopieren',pm=>{if(srcK==='all')pm.t[t].seq=deep(S0);else pm.t[t].seq.q[dstK]=deep(S0.q[srcK]);});
lcdShow('SEQ · KOPIEREN',(src.name||'Sound')+' T'+(srcT?2:1)+(srcK==='all'?' alle Sequenzen':' SEQ '+(srcK+1)+' → SEQ '+(dstK+1))+' · ↶ = zurück');return true;
}
function seqToolsPop(anchor,tr){

},{name:'mkseq'});

function wheels(show){
const A=EL.kbArea;let wh=document.getElementById('mkWh');
if(!show){if(wh)wh.hidden=true;A.classList.remove('mkw');return;}
if(!wh){
wh=el('div','','<div class="mkwheel" data-w="bend" role="slider" aria-label="PITCH-Rad"><span class="lg">PITCH</span><div class="mwell"><div class="mwhl"><i class="mrid"></i></div><i class="mmid"></i></div></div>'+
'<div class="mkwheel" data-w="mod" role="slider" aria-label="MOD-Rad"><span class="lg">MOD</span><div class="mwell"><div class="mwhl"><i class="mrid"></i></div></div></div>');
wh.id='mkWh';A.insertBefore(wh,A.firstChild);
wh.querySelectorAll('.mkwheel').forEach(wheelGesture);
}
wh.hidden=false;A.classList.add('mkw');wheelPaint(R.selT);
}
function wheelPaint(i){
const wh=document.getElementById('mkWh');if(!wh||wh.hidden||i!==R.selT)return;
for(const e of wh.querySelectorAll('.mkwheel')){const w=e.dataset.w,v=mkWheelVal(i,w),n=w==='bend'?(v+1)/2:v;
e.style.setProperty('--n',n.toFixed(4));e.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(Math.round(w==='bend'?v*63:v*127)));}
}
function wheelGesture(e){
guardTouch(e);let g=null;const w=e.dataset.w;
on(e,'pointerdown',ev=>{if(ev.button>0||g)return;ev.preventDefault();ev.stopPropagation();try{e.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId);}catch(_){}
const r=e.querySelector('.mwell').getBoundingClientRect();g={id:ev.pointerId,y0:ev.clientY,v0:mkWheelVal(R.selT,w),span:Math.max(20,r.height-8)};
e.classList.add('press');cancelAnimationFrame(e._ra);});
on(e,'pointermove',ev=>{if(!g||ev.pointerId!==g.id)return;
const dy=(g.y0-ev.clientY)/g.span;let v=w==='bend'?g.v0+dy*2:g.v0+dy;
if(w==='bend'&&Math.abs(v)<.02)v=0;
mkWheelSet(R.selT,w,v,0);wheelPaint(R.selT);
const x=mkWheelVal(R.selT,w);if(ED.lcd)lcdShow(w==='bend'?'PITCH-RAD':'MOD-RAD',w==='bend'?ft(Math.round(x*63),1):String(Math.round(x*127)));
});
const end=ev=>{if(!g||ev.pointerId!==g.id)return;g=null;e.classList.remove('press');
if(w==='bend'){
const i=R.selT,v0=mkWheelVal(i,'bend'),t0=performance.now();mkWheelSet(i,'bend',0,.08);
const o=mkWheelOf(i);o.bend=v0;const st=()=>{const x=Math.min(1,(performance.now()-t0)/80);o.bend=v0*(1-x);wheelPaint(i);if(x<1)e._ra=requestAnimationFrame(st);else{o.bend=0;wheelPaint(i);}};st();
}};
on(e,'pointerup',end);on(e,'pointercancel',end);
}
const WV={built:new Map(),part:new Map(),ctx:new WeakMap(),q:[],wait:new Map(),run:false,posts:0,built0:0,at:{}};
function wvCtx(c){let o=WV.ctx.get(c);if(!o){o={sent:new Set(),bytes:0,asked:new Map()};WV.ctx.set(c,o);}return o;}
function wvStep(n){
if(WV.built.has(n))return true;
const oc=MKTAB.isOct(n),ns=oc?8:1;
let P=WV.part.get(n);if(!P){P={d:new Float32Array(MKTAB.SET*ns),j:0,B:null,R:[]};WV.part.set(n,P);}
if(!P.B)P.B=MKTAB.builder(oc?{n,oct:P.j}:{n});
if(P.B.step()){P.d.set(P.B.buf,P.j*MKTAB.SET);P.R.push([P.B.lo,P.B.hi]);P.B=null;P.j++;}
if(P.j<ns)return false;
WV.part.delete(n);WV.built.set(n,{n,r:MKTAB.r(n),o:oc?1:0,d:P.d.buffer,L:MKTAB.NL,R:P.R,e:oc?MKTAB.envMsg(n):null});return true;
}
function wvPost(E,n){
if(!E||E.dead||!E.node)return;const w=WV.built.get(n);if(!w)return;const C=wvCtx(E.node.context);
try{E.node.port.postMessage({mkw:{n:w.n,r:w.r,o:w.o,d:w.d,L:w.L,R:w.R,e:w.e}});}catch(e){logErr(e);return;}
C.sent.add(n);C.bytes+=w.d.byteLength;WV.posts++;WV.at[n]=performance.now();
}
function wvLive(){const c=AU.ctx;if(!c)return null;for(let i=0;i<AU.synth.length;i++){const E=AU.synth[i],s_=sndOf(i);if(E&&!E.dead&&E.node&&E.node.context===c&&s_&&s_.engine==='mk')return E;}return null;}
function wvSlice(){
WV.run=false;try{tickAhead(.3);}catch(e){}
const t0=performance.now(),lim=WV.wait.size?6.5:5;
while(performance.now()-t0<lim){
const n=WV.wait.size?WV.wait.keys().next().value:WV.q.length?WV.q[0]:0;if(!n)break;
if(!wvStep(n))continue;
if(WV.wait.has(n)){for(const E of WV.wait.get(n))if(!wvCtx(E.node.context).sent.has(n))wvPost(E,n);WV.wait.delete(n);}
const i=WV.q.indexOf(n);if(i>=0)WV.q.splice(i,1);
const L=wvLive();if(L&&!wvCtx(L.node.context).sent.has(n))wvPost(L,n);
}
wvKick();
}
let WVMC=null;
function wvKick(){if(WV.run||(!WV.q.length&&!WV.wait.size))return;WV.run=true;
if(WV.wait.size){try{if(!WVMC){WVMC=new MessageChannel();WVMC.port1.onmessage=wvSlice;}WVMC.port2.postMessage(0);}catch(e){setTimeout(wvSlice,0);}}
else if(typeof requestIdleCallback!=='function')setTimeout(wvSlice,30);else requestIdleCallback(wvSlice,{timeout:250});}
const wv={
need(E,n){
if(!E||!E.node||!(n>=1&&n<=64))return;const C=wvCtx(E.node.context);
if(!C.sent.has(n)){
if(!WV.built.has(n)&&!MKTAB.isOct(n)){try{tickAhead(.3);}catch(e){}while(!wvStep(n));}
if(WV.built.has(n))wvPost(E,n);
else{const a=WV.wait.get(n)||[];if(!a.includes(E))a.push(E);WV.wait.set(n,a);if(WV.run&&WV.wait.size===1)WV.run=false;wvKick();}
}
if(!WV.built0&&E.node.context===AU.ctx){WV.built0=1;for(let k=1;k<=64;k++)if(!MKTAB.isOct(k)&&!WV.built.has(k))WV.q.push(k);wvKick();}
},
pull(E,n){
if(!E||!E.node||!(n>=1&&n<=64))return;const C=wvCtx(E.node.context),now=performance.now();
if(!C.sent.has(n)){this.need(E,n);return;}
const a=C.asked.get(E)||{};if(a[n]&&now-a[n]>600){wvPost(E,n);delete a[n];}else a[n]=a[n]||now;C.asked.set(E,a);
},
now(E,n){if(!E||!E.node||!(n>=1&&n<=64))return;while(!wvStep(n));WV.wait.delete(n);if(!wvCtx(E.node.context).sent.has(n))wvPost(E,n);},
info(c){const C=c?wvCtx(c):{sent:new Set(),bytes:0};return{tables:C.sent.size,tableBytes:C.bytes,built:WV.built.size,posts:WV.posts};},
all(){for(let k=1;k<=64;k++)if(!WV.built.has(k)&&!WV.q.includes(k))WV.q.push(k);wvKick();},
WV};
async function probe(cases,o){
o=o||{};cases=cases&&cases.length?cases:['kern'];
if(!AU.ctx)ensureAudio();
const c=AU.ctx;if(!c||SynthHost.state!=='ready'){await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,1500));}
if(SynthHost.state!=='ready')return{app:VERSION,err:'Studio-Klang (AudioWorklet) nicht verfügbar'};
const sec_=o.sec||10,out={app:VERSION,ua:navigator.userAgent,sr:c.sampleRate,baseLatency:c.baseLatency||0,cases:[]};
const net0=(window.performance&&performance.getEntriesByType)?performance.getEntriesByType('resource').length:0;
PROBE.stop=false;
for(const fall of cases){
if(o.onCase)o.onCase(cases.indexOf(fall));
const sink=c.createGain();sink.gain.value=0;sink.connect(c.destination);
if(fall==='north'){const r=await mdProbe(c,sink,sec_,o,()=>PROBE.stop);try{sink.disconnect();}catch(e){}out.cases.push(r);if(PROBE.stop){r.abgebrochen=true;break;}continue;}
const mkSnd=(o1,o2)=>{const snd=factorySound(INIT_IDS.mk);snd.p.mk=SQMK.normMk(Object.assign(snd.p.mk,{t:[Object.assign(snd.p.mk.t[0],{o1,o2,
mix:{o1:127,o2:90,n:30},f:{type:0,cut:80,res:90,eg:30,vel:0,kbd:24},a:Object.assign(snd.p.mk.t[0].a,{dist:1}),vib:20,
p:[{s:0,d:0,i:3},{s:2,d:4,i:20},{s:3,d:0,i:0},{s:4,d:5,i:0}]}),snd.p.mk.t[1]]}));return snd;};
const SN=[mkSnd({w:5,c1:0,c2:44},{w:1,mod:2,semi:7,tune:0}),mkSnd({w:3,c1:64,c2:30},{w:1,mod:2,semi:7,tune:0})];
if(fall==='seq'){const V=(a,b)=>Array.from({length:16},(_,i)=>Math.round(a+(b-a)*i/15));SN.forEach(snd=>{snd.p.mk.t[0].seq={on:1,last:16,type:2,run:1,ks:2,res:3,q:[{k:11,m:0,v:V(-40,40)},{k:1,m:1,v:V(-12,12)},{k:16,m:0,v:V(30,-30)}]};snd.p.mk=SQMK.normMk(snd.p.mk);});}
if(fall==='fx'||fall==='seq')SN.forEach((snd,k)=>{snd.p.mk=SQMK.normMk(Object.assign(snd.p.mk,{mfx:{type:k?0:2,spd:40,dep:100},dly:{type:0,sync:1,time:64,note:5,dep:64},eq:{lf:200,lg:6,hf:8000,hg:-4}}));});
if(fall==='voc')SN.forEach(snd=>{const t=snd.p.mk.t[0];t.o1={w:0,c1:0,c2:0};t.mix={o1:127,o2:0,n:20};
snd.p.mk=SQMK.normMk(Object.assign(snd.p.mk,{mode:3,voc:Object.assign(snd.p.mk.voc,{res:64,msrc:2,mint:20,efs:40,hpl:30})}));});
let vsrc=null,vmx=null;
if(fall==='voc'){const sr=c.sampleRate,x=mkVowel({f0:110,v:['a','e','i','o','u'],seg:.5,sec:2.5,sr,rms:-20,vib:20}),b=c.createBuffer(1,x.length,sr);b.getChannelData(0).set(x);
vsrc=c.createBufferSource();vsrc.buffer=b;vsrc.loop=true;vmx=c.createChannelMerger(2);vsrc.connect(vmx,0,1);vsrc.start();}
const Es=[];for(let k=0;k<4;k++){const snd=SN[k&1],E=SynthHost.track(c,sink,wkFlat(snd),[],null,snd.p.mk);E.mkWheel({mw0:.5});E.tempo(S.bpm,null);mkwFeed(E,snd);if(vmx)vmx.connect(E.node);Es.push(E);}
const PS_=fall==='panel'?panelScript(sec_*1000):null;
const rep=[];let fr=0;const fps=[];let lastF=performance.now(),gaps=0,lt=0,po=null;
try{po=new PerformanceObserver(l=>{for(const x of l.getEntries())if(x.duration>50)lt++;});po.observe({entryTypes:['longtask']});}catch(e){po=null;}
const onRep=()=>{const L=Es.map(E=>E.load||0);rep.push({sum:L.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),per:L});};
Es.forEach(E=>{const f=E.node.port.onmessage;E.node.port.onmessage=ev=>{f(ev);if(ev.data&&ev.data.load!=null&&E===Es[0])setTimeout(onRep,30);};});
const FR=Es.map(()=>({w0:0,n:0,last:0,bad:false}));let qmax=0;
Es.forEach((E,i)=>{E.onRep=m=>{const F=FR[i];if(m.qmax>qmax)qmax=m.qmax;
if(!F.w0){F.w0=m.wall;F.last=m.wall;return;}
F.n+=m.fr;if(m.wall-F.last>1600||F.n<.995*c.sampleRate*(m.wall-F.w0)/1000-2048)F.bad=true;F.last=m.wall;};});
const t0=performance.now();let id=1e8;
const chord=[[48,55,60,64],[45,52,57,60],[41,48,53,57],[43,50,55,59]];
let k=0;
const raf=()=>{const n=performance.now();fps.push(1000/Math.max(1,n-lastF));if(n-lastF>50)gaps++;lastF=n;fr++;if(n-t0<sec_*1000&&!PROBE.stop)requestAnimationFrame(raf);};
requestAnimationFrame(raf);
if(fall==='arp'){const stp=ARPC.RES[0]*4*60/S.bpm;let tn=c.currentTime+.05,cc=0;
while(performance.now()-t0<sec_*1000&&!PROBE.stop){
while(tn<c.currentTime+.15){const ch=chord[Math.floor(cc/24)%4].map(p=>({p}));
Es.forEach((E,j)=>{if(cc%24===0)E.keys(9,tn);const n=ARPC.arpPattern(0,2,ch,cc+j*3,0,4)[0],x=++id;E.on(x,n.p,.8,tn);E.off(x,tn+stp*.75);});
tn+=stp;cc++;}
for(const E of Es)E.flush();
if(o.onTick)o.onTick((performance.now()-t0)/1000/sec_);
await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,40));
}}
while(fall!=='arp'&&performance.now()-t0<sec_*1000&&!PROBE.stop){
const ch=chord[k++%4],tt=c.currentTime+.05;
for(const E of Es)for(const nt of ch){const x=++id;E.on(x,nt,.8,tt);E.off(x,tt+.45);}
for(const E of Es)E.flush();
if(o.onTick)o.onTick((performance.now()-t0)/1000/sec_);
await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,500));
}
if(po)try{po.disconnect();}catch(e){}
const tEnd=Date.now(),framesOk=!PROBE.stop?FR.every(F=>F.w0&&!F.bad&&tEnd-F.last<=1600):FR.every(F=>!F.bad);
for(const E of Es){E.panic();E.flush();E.onRep=null;}
await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,120));
for(const E of Es)E.dispose();try{sink.disconnect();}catch(e){}
if(vsrc){try{vsrc.stop();vsrc.disconnect();vmx.disconnect();}catch(e){}}
const sums=rep.map(x=>x.sum).sort((a,b)=>a-b),med=sums.length?sums[sums.length>>1]:0,fm=fps.slice().sort((a,b)=>a-b);
const ios=/iP(hone|ad|od)/.test(navigator.userAgent);
const r={fall,voices:4,load_med:+med.toFixed(4),load_max:+(sums.length?sums[sums.length-1]:0).toFixed(4),
load_per_engine:rep.length?rep[rep.length>>1].per.map(x=>+x.toFixed(4)):[],frames_ok:framesOk,quantum_max_ms:+qmax.toFixed(2),
fps_med:+(fm.length?fm[fm.length>>1]:0).toFixed(1),reports:rep.length};
if(ios){r.raf_gaps=gaps;r.raf_capped=r.fps_med<35;}else r.longtasks=po?lt:null;
if(PS_){PS_.stop();r.panel=PS_.res;}
try{r.north_load=+MDX.cpu().toFixed(4);}catch(e){}
out.cases.push(r);
if(PROBE.stop){r.abgebrochen=true;break;}
}
const c0=out.cases[0]||{};Object.assign(out,{fall:c0.fall,voices:c0.voices,load_med:c0.load_med,load_per_engine:c0.load_per_engine,load_max:c0.load_max,frames_ok:c0.frames_ok,
quantum_max_ms:c0.quantum_max_ms,fps_med:c0.fps_med,longtasks:c0.longtasks,raf_gaps:c0.raf_gaps,raf_capped:c0.raf_capped,
net:(window.performance&&performance.getEntriesByType?performance.getEntriesByType('resource').length:0)-net0});
PROBE.last=out;return out;
}
const PROBE={stop:false,last:null};
function panelScript(ms){
const res={offen:!!(P.el&&P.el.fr&&P.el.fr.isConnected&&P.i>=0),schritte:0};if(!res.offen)return{res,stop(){}};
const i=P.i,keep=deep(sndOf(i)),v0={ansicht:P.ansicht,halt:P.halt,mode:P.mode,page:P.page,tab:ED.tab},t0=performance.now();let raf=0,last=-1;
const KS=['f.cut','f.res','k8','k9'],plan=[];
for(let k=0;k<4;k++)plan.push([.2+.4*k,'knob',KS[k]]);
plan.push([1.9,'zoom',4]);for(let n=1;n<=13;n++)plan.push([2.2+.33*n,'halt',n]);
plan.push([6.8,'map']);for(let n=1;n<=6;n++)plan.push([7+.25*n,'page',n*2]);plan.push([8.8,'exit']);plan.push([9.1,'spiel']);
const tick=()=>{
const t=(performance.now()-t0)/1000;if(t*1000>ms||PROBE.stop||P.i!==i)return;
for(const[at,a,x]of plan){if(at>t||at<=last)continue;last=at;res.schritte++;
try{if(a==='zoom')zoomTo(x);else if(a==='halt')zoomTo(x);else if(a==='map')toOverview();else if(a==='page'){P.mode='edit';stepKey(x);}else if(a==='exit'){P.mode='spiel';renderSoon();}else if(a==='spiel')tabTo('spiel');}catch(e){logErr(e);}}
const cur=plan.filter(p=>p[1]==='knob'&&t>=p[0]&&t<p[0]+.38).pop();
if(cur){const d=defOf(cur[2]);if(d){const[lo,hi]=range(d);setv(d,lo+(hi-lo)*(.5+.4*Math.sin((t-cur[0])*16)),true);P.lastKnob=d;if(PL.ui&&PL.ui.knob[cur[2]])paintKnob(cur[2]);showVal(d);}}
raf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
};
raf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
return{res,stop(){cancelAnimationFrame(raf);try{if(sndOf(i))applySound(i,keep);Object.assign(P,{ansicht:v0.ansicht,halt:v0.halt,mode:v0.mode,page:v0.page});if(ED.tab!==v0.tab)tabTo(v0.tab);else if(P.el&&P.el.T){applyView(false);renderSoon();}}catch(e){logErr(e);}}};
}
function probeSheet(){
openPop(null,p=>{
p.innerHTML=`<h4>Leistungs-Probe · ${MK_LABEL}</h4><p class="small">4 unhörbare Klangerzeuger spielen je 4 Stimmen, 10 s. Gemessen wird, wie viel Rechenzeit sie brauchen. Kein Netz, kein Mikrofon.</p>`+
'<div class="plist"><button class="rb" data-a="go"><b>Probe starten</b><small>Fälle „Kern“ (Speicherwelle, Sinus×), „+FX“ (dazu MOD-FX, DELAY, EQ), „+Arp“ (Arpeggiator 1/24), „+Seq“ (dazu drei Sequenzen je Stimme), „Vocoder“, „Bedienfeld“ (bei offenem Bedienfeld: Regler, Zoom, alle Halte, Seiten) und „'+MD_NAME+'“ (Drum-Synthese, 12 Kanäle, 16tel), je 10 s</small></button></div><pre class="mkpr small"></pre>'+
'<div class="plist"><button class="rb" data-a="copy" disabled><b>Ergebnis kopieren</b></button><button class="rb" data-a="file" disabled><b>Als Datei</b></button><button class="rb" data-a="stop"><b>Abbrechen</b></button></div>';
guardTouch(p);const pre=p.querySelector('.mkpr');
onTap(p,async b=>{
const a=b.dataset.a;
if(a==='stop'){PROBE.stop=true;closePop();return;}
if(a==='copy'){try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(PROBE.last,null,1));toast('Ergebnis kopiert');}catch(e){toast('Kopieren geht hier nicht – „Als Datei“');}return;}
if(a==='file'){try{deliverNow('synquenzer-leistungs-probe',new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(PROBE.last,null,1)),'json','Leistungs-Probe');}catch(e){logErr(e);}return;}
b.disabled=true;pre.textContent='läuft …';
let ci=0;const r=await probe(['kern','fx','arp','seq','voc','panel','north'],{onTick:x=>{pre.textContent='läuft … '+Math.round((Math.min(1,x)+ci)/7*100)+' %';},onCase:k=>{ci=k;}});
pre.textContent=JSON.stringify(r,null,1);p.querySelectorAll('button[data-a="copy"],button[data-a="file"]').forEach(x=>{x.disabled=false;});b.disabled=false;
},'button[data-a]');
},{name:'mkprobe'});
}
function randomizeMk(i){
const s_=deep(sndOf(i)),t=s_.p.mk.t[0],R_=Math.random,I=(a,b)=>a+Math.floor(R_()*(b-a+1)),pick=a=>a[Math.floor(R_()*a.length)];
t.o1.w=pick([0,0,1,1,2,3,4,5,5,6]);t.o1.c1=R_()<.5?0:I(0,110);t.o1.c2=R_()<.6?0:I(0,80);
if(t.o1.w===5)t.o1.c2=I(0,127);else if(t.o1.w===6){t.o1.c1=I(16,48);t.o1.c2=I(60,127);}else if(t.o1.w===3)t.o1.c1=I(0,90);
if(t.o1.w===1&&t.o1.c1+t.o1.c2>100){t.o1.c1=Math.min(t.o1.c1,100);t.o1.c2=100-t.o1.c1;}
t.o2.w=I(0,2);t.o2.mod=R_()<.7?0:I(1,3);t.o2.semi=pick([0,7,12,-12]);t.o2.tune=R_()<.5?0:I(-8,8);
t.mix.o1=I(0,127);t.mix.o2=I(0,127);t.mix.n=R_()<.7?0:I(0,40);
if(Math.max(t.mix.o1,t.mix.o2)<64)t[R_()<.5?'mix':'mix'][R_()<.5?'o1':'o2']=I(64,127);
t.f.type=R_()<.8?I(0,1):I(0,3);t.f.cut=I(40,120);t.f.res=I(0,90);t.f.eg=I(-20,63);t.f.kbd=I(0,48);
if(t.f.type>=2){t.f.cut=I(35,80);t.f.eg=I(-10,30);t.f.kbd=I(24,48);}
for(const e of['e1','e2']){t[e].a=R_()<.7?I(0,40):I(40,e==='e2'?72:90);t[e].d=I(20,100);t[e].s=I(0,127);t[e].r=I(10,90);}
if(t.e2.s<20&&t.e2.d<50)t.e2.d=I(50,100);
s_.p.mk=SQMK.normMk(s_.p.mk);UI.ab=null;applySound(i,s_);
}

function k1JumpPath(path){
const m=/^t\.([01])\./.exec(path);if(m)P.tim=+m[1]===1&&dualOn()?2:1;
const rel=m?'t.0.'+path.slice(4):path;
if(P.view)viewClose();
if(P.tab!=='geraet')tabTo('geraet');
const e=ELS.find(x=>x.act&&bindOf(x.id)===path),pg=pageOf(rel);
if(pg){P.mode='edit';goPage(pg[0],pg[1]);}
if(e&&e.halt){zoomTo(e.halt,true);P.lastKnob=defOf(e.id);}
renderSoon();const d=pg?curItem():P.lastKnob;if(d)lcdOf(d);
return{halt:e?e.halt||null:null,page:pg?pg[0]:null};
}

(function(){
'use strict';
const F=SQ_MK_FACTORY,BL='ABCDEFGH';
const RA=[null,['hart','Synth, hart','chord','stab'],['arp','Arpeggio','seq','chord'],['flaeche','Fläche','pad','pad'],['bass','Bass','bass','bass'],
['lead','Lead','lead','lead'],['seq','Sequenz','seq','chord'],['perk','Glocke, Perkussion','keys','bell'],['tasten','Tasten','keys','keys'],
['bass','Bass','bass','bass'],['bewegung','Bewegung','seq','chord'],['chor','Streicher, Chor','pad','pad'],['lead','Lead','lead','lead'],
['blaeser','Bläser, weich','chord','chord'],['wseq','Wellen-Sequenz','seq','pad'],['bass','Bass','bass','bass'],['effekt','Effekt','fx','fx']];
const RFX=['effekt','Effekt','fx','fx'],RVOC=['vocoder','Vocoder','chord','chord'],RIN=['eingang','Eingang','fx','lead'];
function roleOf(b,s){if(b===8){if(s===2||s===7)return RFX;if(s>=9&&s<=14)return RVOC;if(s===15)return RIN;}return RA[s];}
const CAT={bass:'bass',lead:'lead',chord:'poly',pad:'flaeche',keys:'pluck',seq:'sequenz',fx:'effekt'};
const lab=(b,s)=>BL[b-1]+String(s).padStart(2,'0');
const idOf=(b,s)=>'f-mk-'+BL[b-1].toLowerCase()+String(s).padStart(2,'0');
function leaves(){
const L=[],init=SQMK.init();
const walk=(o,pre)=>{for(const k of Object.keys(o)){if(!pre&&(k==='v'||k==='usc'||k==='vmax'))continue;const v=o[k],p=pre?pre+'.'+k:k;if(p==='voc.hold')continue;
if(Array.isArray(v))v.forEach((x,i)=>(x&&typeof x==='object'?walk(x,p+'.'+i):L.push([p+'.'+i,x])));
else if(v&&typeof v==='object')walk(v,p);else L.push([p,p==='eq.lg'||p==='eq.hg'?v*2:v]);}};
walk(init,'');
for(let i=0;i<16;i++)L.push(['voc.hold.'+i,0]);
L.push(['holdOn',0],['mw',0]);
return L;
}
let LV=null,LVP=null,INIT_S='';
function prepLV(){if(LV)return;LV=leaves();LVP=LV.map(l=>l[0].split('.'));INIT_S=JSON.stringify(SQMK.init());}
function build(diffs){
const pm=JSON.parse(INIT_S);let hold=null,holdOn=0,mw=0;
for(const[i,d]of diffs){
const p=LV[i][0],v=LV[i][1]+d;
if(p.startsWith('voc.hold.'))(hold||(hold=new Array(16).fill(0)))[+p.slice(9)]=v;
else if(p==='holdOn')holdOn=v;else if(p==='mw')mw=v;
else{const ks=LVP[i];let o=pm;for(let k=0;k<ks.length-1;k++)o=o[ks[k]];o[ks[ks.length-1]]=p==='eq.lg'||p==='eq.hg'?v/2:v;}
}
pm.voc.hold=holdOn?hold||new Array(16).fill(0):null;
return{mk:SQMK.normMk(pm),mw:Math.max(0,Math.min(127,mw))/127};
}
const BANKS=new Array(8).fill(null),STAT={ms:new Array(8).fill(0)};
function decodeBank(b){
if(BANKS[b-1])return BANKS[b-1];
try{tickAhead(.3);}catch(e){}
const t0=performance.now();
prepLV();
const bin=atob(F.B[b-1]||''),u=new Uint8Array(bin.length);for(let i=0;i<bin.length;i++)u[i]=bin.charCodeAt(i);
const salt=u.length?u[0]:0;let k=1;
const rd=()=>{let v=0,m=1,x;do{x=k<u.length?u[k++]^salt:0;v+=(x&127)*m;m*=128;}while(x&128);return v;};
const out=[];
for(let s=0;s<16;s++){
const n=k<u.length?rd():0,diffs=[];let idx=-1;
for(let j=0;j<n;j++){idx+=rd()+1;const z=rd();if(idx<LV.length)diffs.push([idx,z&1?-(z+1)/2:z/2]);}
out.push(build(diffs));
}
STAT.ms[b-1]=performance.now()-t0;
return(BANKS[b-1]=out);
}
function decodeAll(){
return new Promise(res=>{let b=1;const next=()=>{if(b>8){res(STAT.ms.slice());return;}decodeBank(b++);setTimeout(next,30);};next();});
}
const NAMES=F.N.map(x=>(x?x.split('|'):[]));
const FREC=new Map();
function facRec(b,s){
if(b===8&&s===16)return FACTORY_BY_ID.get(INIT_IDS.mk);
const id=idOf(b,s);let r=FREC.get(id);if(r)return r;
const R_=roleOf(b,s),lib=R_[2];
r={id,name:NAMES[b-1][s-1]||lab(b,s),cat:CAT[lib]||'eigene',engine:'mk',ev:1,demo:R_[3],ref:lab(b,s)+' · '+R_[1],
tip:(F.T[b-1]||[])[s-1]||'',role:lib,prole:R_[0],bank:b,slot:s,mods:[],fx:INIT_FX(),bus:null};
Object.defineProperty(r,'p',{enumerable:true,get(){const d=decodeBank(b)[s-1];return Object.assign({},INIT_VA_P,{level:.457,mw:d.mw,mk:deep(d.mk)});}});
FREC.set(id,r);return r;
}
(function register(){
const add=[];
for(let b=1;b<=8;b++)for(let s=1;s<=16;s++){if(b===8&&s===16)continue;const r=facRec(b,s);FACTORY_BY_ID.set(r.id,r);add.push(r);}
const k=SYNTH_LIB.findIndex(x=>x.cat==='init');SYNTH_LIB.splice(k<0?SYNTH_LIB.length:k,0,...add);
try{OHASH.key='';}catch(e){}
prepLV();
})();
const ownAt=(b,s)=>(LIB.ok?LIB.lib.all():[]).find(x=>x.engine==='mk'&&x.bank===b&&x.slot===s)||null;
const progRec=(b,s)=>ownAt(b,s)||facRec(b,s);
const bankList=b=>{const o=[];for(let s=1;s<=16;s++)o.push(progRec(b,s));return o;};
const P={bank:1,last:null};
function cur(t){
const tr=S.tracks[t];if(!tr||tr.kind!=='synth'||!tr.sound||tr.sound.engine!=='mk')return null;
const o=tr.sound.origin||'';let b=0,s=0;
const m=/^f-mk-([a-h])(\d\d)$/.exec(o);
if(m){b=BL.toLowerCase().indexOf(m[1])+1;s=+m[2];}
else if(o===INIT_IDS.mk){b=8;s=16;}
else if(o.startsWith('u-')){const r=LIB.ok?LIB.lib.get(o):null;if(r&&r.bank){b=r.bank;s=r.slot;}}
if(!b||s<1||s>16)return null;
const snd=tr.sound;return{bank:b,slot:s,label:lab(b,s),name:snd.name,text:lab(b,s)+':'+snd.name,own:o.startsWith('u-'),get edited(){return soundEdited(snd);}};
}
const okBS=(b,s)=>Number.isInteger(b)&&Number.isInteger(s)&&b>=1&&b<=8&&s>=1&&s<=16;
function headRefresh(t){
if(R.editor==='synth'&&t===R.selT){UI.dirty.edHead=true;if(ED.hdr)ED.hdr.key=null;try{if(window.MKUI&&MKUI.progRefresh)MKUI.progRefresh();}catch(e){logErr(e);}}
if(R.editor==='browser')brRefresh();
}
function select(t,b,s){
const tr=S.tracks[t];if(!tr||tr.kind!=='synth'||!okBS(b,s)||nvTr(tr))return false;
const r=progRec(b,s),L=lab(b,s);
previewStop();
soundEdit(t,'Programm '+L,()=>loadPreset(t,r.id));
P.bank=b;P.last={bank:b,slot:s};
const txt=L+':'+r.name;
lcdShow(b===8&&s===15&&!r.id.startsWith('u-')?'Eingang wählen: GLOBAL 1A':'Programm',txt);
toast(txt,[{label:'Rückgängig',fn:()=>undo()}]);
return true;
}
function step(t,dir){
const c=cur(t)||P.last,i=c?(c.bank-1)*16+c.slot-1:-1,d=dir<0?-1:1;
let n=i+d;
if(i<0)n=d>0?0:127;
if(n<0||n>127){lcdShow('Programm',n<0?'Anfang · A01':'Ende · H16');return false;}
return select(t,Math.floor(n/16)+1,n%16+1);
}
function bankStep(dir){const c=cur(R.selT);if(!P.touched&&c)P.bank=c.bank;P.touched=1;if(!dir)return P.bank;P.bank=Math.max(1,Math.min(8,P.bank+(dir<0?-1:1)));lcdShow('Bank',BL[P.bank-1]+' · SELECT 1–16 wählt das Programm');return P.bank;}
async function write(t,b,s,name){
const tr=S.tracks[t];if(!tr||tr.kind!=='synth'||tr.sound.engine!=='mk'||!okBS(b,s)||nvTr(tr))return null;
if(!LIB.ok)await libReady();
const occ=ownAt(b,s),before=soundSnapshot(t),L=lab(b,s);
const snd=deep(tr.sound);snd.bank=b;snd.slot=s;
const saved=await LIB.lib.save(snd,{mode:'new',name:(name&&String(name).trim())||tr.sound.name});
if(occ)await LIB.lib.put(occ.id,Object.assign(deep(occ),{bank:0,slot:0}));
const ids=[saved.id].concat(occ?[occ.id]:[]),was={};was[saved.id]=null;if(occ)was[occ.id]=occ;
tr.sound.origin=saved.id;tr.sound.name=saved.name;UI.ab=null;UI.sndVer++;markDirty();UI.dirty.strip=UI.dirty.grid=true;
undoPush({kind:'group',label:'Sichern auf '+L,items:[{kind:'mkslot',ids,before:JSON.stringify(was)},{kind:'sound',track:t,before}]});
P.bank=b;P.last={bank:b,slot:s};
libChanged();headRefresh(t);
const txt=L+':'+saved.name;
lcdShow('Gesichert auf Platz',txt+(occ?' · „'+occ.name+'“ bleibt in Eigene':''));
toast('Geschrieben: '+txt,[{label:'Rückgängig',fn:()=>undo()}]);
return deep(saved);
}
function restore(t,b,s){
if(!okBS(b,s))return false;
const occ=ownAt(b,s),L=lab(b,s),f=facRec(b,s);
if(!occ){lcdShow('Werksklang',L+' spielt schon den Werksklang „'+f.name+'“');return false;}
const items=[{kind:'mkslot',ids:[occ.id],before:mkSlotSnap([occ.id])}];
LIB.lib.put(occ.id,Object.assign(deep(occ),{bank:0,slot:0})).catch(logErr);
const tr=S.tracks[t];
if(tr&&tr.kind==='synth'&&tr.sound.origin===occ.id){const bs=soundSnapshot(t);loadPreset(t,f.id);items.push({kind:'sound',track:t,before:bs});if(UI.mkInJoin&&UI.mkInJoin.track===t){items.push(UI.mkInJoin);UI.mkInJoin=null;}}
undoPush({kind:'group',label:'Werksklang '+L,items});
libChanged();headRefresh(t);
lcdShow('Werksklang zurück',L+':'+f.name+' · „'+occ.name+'“ bleibt in Eigene');
toast('Werksklang zurück: '+L+':'+f.name,[{label:'Rückgängig',fn:()=>undo()}]);
return true;
}
const label=(b,s)=>(okBS(b,s)?lab(b,s):'');
function names(){const o=[];for(let b=1;b<=8;b++)for(let s=1;s<=16;s++)if(!(b===8&&s===16))o.push(facRec(b,s).name);return o;}
function bankPop(anchor){
hideToast();
openPop(anchor,p=>{
p.classList.add('mk8w');
p.innerHTML='<h4>Bank</h4><div class="mk8g mk8bk">'+[...BL].map((x,k)=>`<button class="rb${BR.bank===k+1?' on':''}" data-b="${k+1}">${x}</button>`).join('')+'</div>'+
`<div class="savrow"><button class="mbtn rb" data-b="0">Alle ${esc(MK_LABEL)}</button></div>`;
guardTouch(p);
onTap(p,x=>{closePop();BR.bank=+x.dataset.b;BR.kp=0;BR.role='alle';BR.more=BR.ren=null;brRefresh();},'button[data-b]');
},{name:'mkbank'});
}
function savePop(anchor,t){
const tr=S.tracks[t];if(!tr)return null;
const c=cur(t)||(P.last?{bank:P.last.bank,slot:P.last.slot}:{bank:1,slot:1}),sel={b:c.bank,s:c.slot};
const o=tr.sound.origin,userOrigin=o&&o.startsWith('u-')&&LIB.ok&&LIB.lib.get(o);
hideToast();
return openPop(anchor,p=>{
p.classList.add('mk8w');
p.innerHTML='<h4>Sichern · Platz wählen</h4><div class="mk8g mk8bk"></div><div class="mk8g mk8sl"></div><p class="mk8i small"></p>';
const inp=el('input','tin');inp.value=tr.sound.name;inp.maxLength=32;inp.setAttribute('aria-label','Name des Sounds');inp.setAttribute('enterkeyhint','done');
const row=el('div','savrow mk8r'),go=el('button','mbtn rb go',''),free=el('button','mbtn rb','Ohne Platz'),ov=el('button','mbtn rb','Überschreiben'),back=el('button','mbtn rb mk8fr','Werksklang zurück');
go.dataset.a='go';free.dataset.a='free';ov.dataset.a='ov';back.dataset.a='back';
row.append(back,ov,free,go);p.append(inp,row);
const bk=p.querySelector('.mk8bk'),sl=p.querySelector('.mk8sl'),inf=p.querySelector('.mk8i');
const paint=()=>{
bk.innerHTML=[...BL].map((x,k)=>`<button class="rb${sel.b===k+1?' on':''}" data-b="${k+1}" aria-label="Bank ${x}">${x}</button>`).join('');
sl.innerHTML=Array.from({length:16},(_,k)=>`<button class="rb${sel.s===k+1?' on':''}${ownAt(sel.b,k+1)?' own':''}" data-s="${k+1}" aria-label="Platz ${lab(sel.b,k+1)}">${k+1}</button>`).join('');
const occ=ownAt(sel.b,sel.s),L=lab(sel.b,sel.s);
inf.textContent=occ?L+' trägt „'+occ.name+'“ (eigener Klang) – er bleibt in Eigene':L+' · Werksklang „'+facRec(sel.b,sel.s).name+'“ wird überdeckt';
go.textContent='Auf '+L+' schreiben';back.hidden=!occ;ov.hidden=!userOrigin;
};
paint();
onTap(p,x=>{
if(x.dataset.b){sel.b=+x.dataset.b;paint();return;}
if(x.dataset.s){sel.s=+x.dataset.s;paint();return;}
const a=x.dataset.a;if(!a)return;
closePop();
if(a==='go')write(t,sel.b,sel.s,inp.value).catch(e=>{logErr(e);toast('Sichern fehlgeschlagen');});
else if(a==='free')saveSound(t,inp.value,'new').then(s_=>toast('Gesichert: „'+s_.name+'“ (Eigene)')).catch(()=>toast('Sichern fehlgeschlagen'));
else if(a==='ov')saveSound(t,inp.value,'overwrite').then(s_=>toast('Überschrieben: „'+s_.name+'“')).catch(()=>toast('Sichern fehlgeschlagen'));
else if(a==='back')restore(t,sel.b,sel.s);
},'button');
on(inp,'keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();closePop();write(t,sel.b,sel.s,inp.value).catch(logErr);}e.stopPropagation();});
let sel1=false;on(inp,'focus',()=>{if(sel1)return;sel1=true;setTimeout(()=>{try{if(document.activeElement===inp)inp.select();}catch(e){}},0);});
},{name:'save'});
}
function arpPhrase(pm){
const a=pm.arp,st=ARPC.RES[Math.max(0,Math.min(5,a.res|0))]*4,N=[48,55,60,63].map(p=>({p})),out=[];
for(let c=0;c*st<8-1e-6;c++)for(const x of ARPC.arpPattern(a.type|0,a.range|0,N,c,12345,4))out.push([c*st,Math.max(.02,a.gate/100*st),x.p,c%4? .7:.9]);
return out;
}
window.MKF={select,step,bankStep,write,restore,cur,label,names,bankList,progRec,facRec,ownAt,bankPop,savePop,arpPhrase,decodeBank,decodeAll,leaves:()=>(LV||(LV=leaves())).map(x=>x.slice()),stat:()=>({ms:STAT.ms.slice(),loaded:BANKS.map(Boolean)}),P,roleOf:(b,s)=>roleOf(b,s).slice()};
})();

(function(){
'use strict';
const K=SQ_MK_MICRO;
const GL=[['TRANCE','TRANCE'],['TECHNO/HOUSE','TECHNO/HOUSE'],['ELECTRONICA','ELECTRONICA'],['D&B/BREAKS','D&B/BREAKS'],['HIPHOP/VINTAGE','HIPHOP/VINTAGE'],['RETRO','RETRO'],['S.E./HIT','S.E./HIT'],['VOCODER','VOCODER']];
const RK={lead:['Lead','lead','lead'],pad:['Fläche','pad','pad'],bass:['Bass','bass','bass'],arp:['Arpeggio','seq','chord'],seq:['Sequenz','seq','chord'],
chord:['Akkord','chord','stab'],keys:['Tasten','keys','keys'],bell:['Glocke','bell','bell'],fx:['Effekt','fx','fx'],hit:['Hit','fx','stab'],
drum:['Drum','fx','seq8'],voc:['Vocoder','chord','chord']};
const CAT={bass:'bass',lead:'lead',chord:'poly',pad:'flaeche',keys:'pluck',seq:'sequenz',fx:'effekt',bell:'glocke'};
const PVH=[46,34,32,57,72,72,80,97,82,89,56,39,33,24,0,0];
const nn=s=>String(s).padStart(2,'0');
const idOf=(g,s)=>'f-mk-k'+g+nn(s);
const lab=(g,s)=>GL[g-1][1]+' '+nn(s);
const NAMES=K.N.map(x=>x.split('|')),ROLES=K.R.map(x=>x.split('|'));
let LV=null,LVP=null,INIT_S='';
function prep(){if(LV)return;LV=MKF.leaves();LVP=LV.map(l=>l[0].split('.'));INIT_S=JSON.stringify(SQMK.init());}
function build(diffs){
const pm=JSON.parse(INIT_S);let hold=null,holdOn=0,mw=0;
for(const[i,d]of diffs){
const p=LV[i][0],v=LV[i][1]+d;
if(p.startsWith('voc.hold.'))(hold||(hold=new Array(16).fill(0)))[+p.slice(9)]=v;
else if(p==='holdOn')holdOn=v;else if(p==='mw')mw=v;
else{const ks=LVP[i];let o=pm;for(let k=0;k<ks.length-1;k++)o=o[ks[k]];o[ks[ks.length-1]]=p==='eq.lg'||p==='eq.hg'?v/2:v;}
}
pm.voc.hold=holdOn?hold||new Array(16).fill(0):null;
return{mk:SQMK.normMk(pm),mw:Math.max(0,Math.min(127,mw))/127};
}
const GEN=new Array(8).fill(null),STAT={ms:new Array(8).fill(0)};
function decodeGenre(g){
if(GEN[g-1])return GEN[g-1];
try{tickAhead(.3);}catch(e){}
const t0=performance.now();prep();
const bin=atob(K.B[g-1]||''),u=new Uint8Array(bin.length);for(let i=0;i<bin.length;i++)u[i]=bin.charCodeAt(i);
const salt=u.length?u[0]:0;let k=1;
const rd=()=>{let v=0,m=1,x;do{x=k<u.length?u[k++]^salt:0;v+=(x&127)*m;m*=128;}while(x&128);return v;};
const out=[];
for(let s=0;s<16;s++){
const n=k<u.length?rd():0,diffs=[];let idx=-1;
for(let j=0;j<n;j++){idx+=rd()+1;const z=rd();if(idx<LV.length)diffs.push([idx,z&1?-(z+1)/2:z/2]);}
out.push(build(diffs));
}
STAT.ms[g-1]=performance.now()-t0;
return(GEN[g-1]=out);
}
function decodeAll(){return new Promise(res=>{let g=1;const next=()=>{if(g>8){res(STAT.ms.slice());return;}decodeGenre(g++);setTimeout(next,30);};next();});}
const REC=new Map();
function rec(g,s){
const id=idOf(g,s);let r=REC.get(id);if(r)return r;
const rk=ROLES[g-1][s-1]||'lead',R_=RK[rk]||RK.lead;
r={id,name:NAMES[g-1][s-1]||lab(g,s),cat:CAT[R_[1]]||'eigene',engine:'mk',ev:1,demo:R_[2],ref:lab(g,s)+' · '+R_[0],
tip:(K.T[g-1]||[])[s-1]||'',role:R_[1],prole:rk,kp:g,kslot:s,mods:[],fx:INIT_FX(),bus:null};
Object.defineProperty(r,'p',{enumerable:true,get(){const d=decodeGenre(g)[s-1];return Object.assign({},INIT_VA_P,{level:.457,mw:d.mw,mk:deep(d.mk)});}});
REC.set(id,r);return r;
}
(function register(){
for(let g=1;g<=8;g++)for(let s=1;s<=16;s++){const r=rec(g,s);FACTORY_BY_ID.set(r.id,r);}
try{OHASH.key='';}catch(e){}
prep();
})();
const of=id=>{const m=/^f-mk-k([1-8])(\d\d)$/.exec(id||'');return m&&+m[2]>=1&&+m[2]<=16?{g:+m[1],s:+m[2]}:null;};
const list=g=>{const o=[];if(g>=1&&g<=8)for(let s=1;s<=16;s++)o.push(rec(g,s));return o;};
function names(){const o=[];for(let g=1;g<=8;g++)for(let s=1;s<=16;s++)o.push(rec(g,s).name);return o;}
function pvFix(snd){const m=snd&&snd.engine==='mk'&&snd.p&&snd.p.mk;if(!m||m.mode!==3||m.voc.hold)return false;m.voc.hold=PVH.slice();return true;}
async function writeRec(r,b,s,name){
if(!r||!of(r.id)||!Number.isInteger(b)||!Number.isInteger(s)||b<1||b>8||s<1||s>16)return null;
if(!LIB.ok)await libReady();
const occ=MKF.ownAt(b,s),L=MKF.label(b,s);
const snd=factorySound(r.id);snd.bank=b;snd.slot=s;
const saved=await LIB.lib.save(snd,{mode:'new',name:(name&&String(name).trim())||r.name});
if(occ)await LIB.lib.put(occ.id,Object.assign(deep(occ),{bank:0,slot:0}));
const ids=[saved.id].concat(occ?[occ.id]:[]),was={};was[saved.id]=null;if(occ)was[occ.id]=occ;
undoPush({kind:'mkslot',ids,before:JSON.stringify(was),label:'Sichern auf '+L});
MKF.P.bank=b;
libChanged();if(R.editor==='browser')brRefresh();
try{if(window.MKUI&&MKUI.progRefresh)MKUI.progRefresh();}catch(e){logErr(e);}
toast('Geschrieben: '+L+':'+saved.name+(occ?' · „'+occ.name+'“ bleibt in Eigene':''),[{label:'Rückgängig',fn:()=>undo()}]);
return deep(saved);
}
function placePop(anchor,r){
if(!r)return null;
const c=MKF.P.last||{bank:MKF.P.bank||1,slot:1},sel={b:c.bank,s:c.slot},BL='ABCDEFGH';
hideToast();
return openPop(anchor,p=>{
p.classList.add('mk8w');
p.innerHTML='<h4>Auf Platz schreiben</h4><div class="mk8g mk8bk"></div><div class="mk8g mk8sl"></div><p class="mk8i small"></p>';
const inp=el('input','tin');inp.value=r.name;inp.maxLength=32;inp.setAttribute('aria-label','Name des Sounds');inp.setAttribute('enterkeyhint','done');
const row=el('div','savrow mk8r'),no=el('button','mbtn rb','Abbrechen'),go=el('button','mbtn rb go','');
no.dataset.a='no';go.dataset.a='go';row.append(no,go);p.append(inp,row);
const bk=p.querySelector('.mk8bk'),sl=p.querySelector('.mk8sl'),inf=p.querySelector('.mk8i');
const paint=()=>{
bk.innerHTML=[...BL].map((x,k)=>`<button class="rb${sel.b===k+1?' on':''}" data-b="${k+1}" aria-label="Bank ${x}">${x}</button>`).join('');
sl.innerHTML=Array.from({length:16},(_,k)=>`<button class="rb${sel.s===k+1?' on':''}${MKF.ownAt(sel.b,k+1)?' own':''}" data-s="${k+1}" aria-label="Platz ${MKF.label(sel.b,k+1)}">${k+1}</button>`).join('');
const occ=MKF.ownAt(sel.b,sel.s),L=MKF.label(sel.b,sel.s);
inf.textContent=occ?L+' trägt „'+occ.name+'“ (eigener Klang) – er bleibt in Eigene':L+' · Werksklang „'+MKF.facRec(sel.b,sel.s).name+'“ wird überdeckt (Werksklang zurück holt ihn wieder)';
go.textContent='Auf '+L+' schreiben';
};
paint();
onTap(p,x=>{
if(x.dataset.b){sel.b=+x.dataset.b;paint();return;}
if(x.dataset.s){sel.s=+x.dataset.s;paint();return;}
